# Rashi on Joshua

רש"י על יהושע

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua)

הפירוש הנפוץ והמוכר ביותר לתורה, המבאר את פשטי המקראות בתוספת הרחבות פרשניות; חובר במאה ה-11.

Most widely-read biblical commentary, compiled in the 11th-century, explaining the simple meaning of the text with interpretive elaborations.

Sections: Chapter-Verse-Comment

Location: Tanakh/Rishonim on Tanakh/Rashi/Prophets

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): The Book of Joshua, Metsudah Publications, 1997 (Segments: 362, License: CC-BY)

Version (En): The Book of Joshua, Metsudah Publications, 1997 (Segments: 362)


## Chapter 1


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:1:1)


**וַיְהִי אַחֲרֵי מוֹת משֶׁה** מְחוּבָּר עַל סֵדֶר הַתּוֹרָה הַמְסַיֶּימֶת בִּפְטִירַת משֶׁה, וְזֶה מְחוּבָּר לָהּ.


**And after the death of Moshe.** This is connected to the chapter of the Torah¹ in *Tanach* such as Ruth, Esther, Yonah that begin with the word וַיְהִי, and it was, that are not connected to any previous event. But here the beginning is definitely connected with the conclusion of *Sefer Devorim* where the death of Moshe is related. which concluded with the passing of Moshe;² and this (Seifer Yehoshua) is connected to it.³

footnotes:
¹ The spirit of prophecy did not rest upon Yehoshua until the thirty day mourning period for Moshe was over. It was then that God began to speak to him and command him to begin the conquest of Eretz Yisroel. Yehoshua was in a state of sadness and the spirit of propecy does not rest upon a person who is sad. There are other Seforim
² Devorim 34:5,6,7,8.
³ Devorim 34:9.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:2:1)


**משֶׁה עַבְדִּי מֵת** וְאִילּוּ הָיָה קַיָּים, בּוֹ הָיִיתִי חָפֵץ. וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ דְּרָשׁוּהוּ (תמורה טז א) עַל שְׁלשֶׁת אֲלָפִים הֲלָכוֹת שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּכְּחוּ בִּימֵי אֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל משֶׁה. בָּא יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְשָׁאַל, אָמַר לוֹ הַקָּבָּ"ה: משֶׁה עַבְדִּי מֵת וְהַתּוֹרָה עַל שְׁמוֹ נִקְרֵאת, לוֹמַר לְךָ אִי אֶפְשָׁר, צֵא וְטוֹרְדָן בְּמִלְחָמָה.


**Moshe, My servant is dead.** But if he were alive,⁴ this is connected to what follows: Since he is dead, you must arouse yourself and cross this Jordan. I would have wanted him [as the leader].⁵ Hashem said that if Moshe was still alive, you would be unable to cross the Jordon because of the decree preventing him from crossing the Jordon. See Bamidbar 20:12; Ibid 27:12,13. Our Sages explain [that this passage is a reference] [to the three thousand laws that were forgotten] during the period of mourning for Moshe. Yehoshua then came and asked.⁶ the Holy One blessed is He, said to him: “Moshe, My servant is dead, and the Torah is called on his name, which is like telling you that it is impossible.⁷ Go and burden them with battles.”⁸

footnotes:
⁴ According to Rashi,
⁵ According to Metsudas Dovid,
⁶ When the Bnei Yisroel realized that they had forgotten so many laws of the Torah, they came to Yehoshua and asked him to teach them the laws, but he too had forgotten many of them. He wanted Hashem to reveal those forgotten laws to him.
⁷ Since the Torah is referred to as תּוֹרַת משֶׁה, the Torah of Moshe, and Moshe is dead, these laws cannot be reenacted.
⁸ God told Yehoshua to get the people occupied with conquering the land. He told him to begin preparations to cross the Jordan and this would cause them to forget about the laws that were lost to them. This refers to the battles of conquest that were required to capture the land.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:3:1)


**כָּל מָקוֹם אֲשֶׁר תִּדְרֹךְ** כַּיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ נֶאֱמַר לְמשֶׁה, וְשָׁנִינוּ בְּסִפְרֵי (פר' עקב): אִם לְלַמֵּד עַל תְּחוּמֵי אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל, הֲרֵי הוּא אוֹמֵר: מֵהַמִּדְבָּר וְהַלְּבָנוֹן הַזֶּה, אִם כֵּן לָמָּה נֶאֱמַר: **כָּל מָקוֹם אֲשֶׁר תִּדְרֹךְ כַּף** אַף חוּצָה לָאָרֶץ, מִשֶּׁתִּכְבְּשׁוּ אֶת הָאָרֶץ, כָּל מַה שֶּׁתִּכְבְּשׁוּ מִחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ יִהְיֶה קָדוֹשׁ וְיִהְיֶה שֶׁלָּכֶם.


**Every place upon which the sole of your feet will tread.** This is similar to what was said to Moshe. Regarding this, we learned in the *Sifre.* ‘If [this verse] is to teach us about the boundaries of Eretz Yisroel, the Torah has already stated:⁹ From the wilderness and the Lebanon etc.¹⁰ If so, why is it said here: **Every place upon which the sole will tread?** This includes the lands outside Eretz Yisroel. After you have conquered the land, everything you conquer outside the land will be sacred and will belong to you.’

footnotes:
⁹ Devorim 11:24.
¹⁰ Therefore we know the boundaries of Eretz Yisroel from the Torah.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:1)


**מֵהַמִּדְבָּר וְהַלְּבָנוֹן הַזֶּה** הוּא מִדְבַּר קָדֵשׁ, מִדְבַּר צִין שֶׁעַל יְדֵי אֱדוֹם, שֶׁהָיָה בְּמִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית וְדֶרֶךְ שָׁם נִכְנְסוּ לָאָרֶץ, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר כ טז): וְהִנֵּה אֲנַחְנוּ בְקָדֵשׁ וְגוֹמֵר, וּמִנַּיִין שֶׁהָיָה בִּדְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם לד ג): וְהָיָה לָכֶם פְּאַת נֶגֶב מִמִּדְבַּר צִין עַל יְדֵי אֱדוֹם.


**From the wilderness and this Lebanon.** The wilderness refers to the wilderness of Kodeish, the wilderness of Tzin, which was adjacient to Edom which was to the south east, the direction from which they entered the land, as it is said: And behold we are in Kodeish.¹¹ How do we know that it was southeast? It is said: ‘Your southern flank will be from the *Tzin* wilderness along Edom.’¹²

footnotes:
¹¹ Bamidbar 20:16.
¹² Bamidbar 34:3.


###### Rashi on Joshua 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:2)


**וְעַד הַנָּהָר הַגָּדוֹל נְהַר פְּרָת** זֶה רָחְבָּהּ מִדָּרוֹם לְצָפוֹן.


**To the great river, the Euphrates river.** This refers to its width from south to north.


###### Rashi on Joshua 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:3)


**כָּל אֶרֶץ הַחִתִּים** בַּכְּלָל הַזֶּה.


This includes all the land of the Chitim.


###### Rashi on Joshua 1:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:4:4)


**וְעַד הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל מְבוֹא הַשָּׁמֶשׁ** לְאָרְכָּהּ, מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב.


**To the Great Sea [westward].** This refers to its length from east to west.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:6:1)


**חֲזַק וֶאֱמָץ** בְּדֶרֶךְ אֶרֶץ, כְּמָה שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר: כִּי אַתָּה תַּנְחִיל.


**Be strong and courageous.** In the physical sense, as He states: “For you will lead this people to inherit the land.”



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:7:1)


**רַק חֲזַק וֶאֱמַץ מְאֹד** בְּתּוֹרָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר: לִשְׁמֹר וְלַעֲשׂוֹת כְּכָל הַתּוֹרָה.


**Just be very strong and courageous.** This was said in reference to the Torah, as He states: ‘To preserve, to act in accordance with all the laws of the Torah.’


###### Rashi on Joshua 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:7:2)


**תַּשְׂכִּיל** תַּצְלִיחַ.


תַּשְּׁכִּיל—You will be successful.¹³).

footnotes:
¹³ People consider that a successful person is a smart person. That is why the word תַּשְׂכִּיל—is associated with the word תַּצְלִיחַ. (Metsudas Tzion



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:8:1)


**סֵפֶר הַתּוֹרָה הַזֶּה** סֵפֶר מִשְׁנֵה תּוֹרָה הָיָה לְפָנָיו.


**This Sefer Torah.¹⁴ implies a specific Book of the Torah. Which one? Thus *Rashi* comments that it refers to Sefer Devorim that was before Him.** The *Sefer Devorim* was before Him.

footnotes:
¹⁴ This Sefer Torah


###### Rashi on Joshua 1:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:8:2)


**וְהָגִיתָ בּוֹ** וְהִתְבּוֹנַנְתָּ בּוֹ, כָּל הִגָּיוֹן שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה בַּלֵּב, כְּמָה דְּאַתְּ אָמַר (תהלים יט טו): וְהֶגְיוֹן לִבִּי לְפָנֶיךָ, (ישעיהו לג יח): לִבְּךָ יֶהְגֶּה אֵימָה.


**And you shall meditate in it.** Every expression of הִגָיוֹן in the Torah refers to the [meditation] of the heart,¹⁵ says that הִגָיוֹן sometimes refers to speech as it is written: And my tongue will speak (תֶּהְגֶה) of Your righteousness (Tehillim 35:28). as it is said, ‘And the thoughts of my heart before you;’¹⁶ ‘Your heart will give forth fearful thoughts.’¹⁷

footnotes:
¹⁵ Metsudas Tzion
¹⁶ Tehillim 19:15.
¹⁷ Yeshayahu 33:18.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:9:1)


**הֲלוֹא צִוִּיתִיךָ חֲזַק וֶאֱמָץ** בְּמִלְחָמָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: אַל תַּעֲרֹץ וְאַל תֵּחָת. וְהֵיכָן צִוָּהוּ בִּימֵי משֶׁה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דברים לא כג): וַיְצַו אֶת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בִּן־נוּן וְגוֹ'.


**I have already commanded you to be stong and courageous.** This was said in reference to actual war, as it is said: ‘Have no fear or trepidation.’ Where was he commanded this? He was so commanded in the time of Moshe, as it is said: ‘and command Yehoshua son of Nun etc.’¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ Devorim 31:23.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:10:1)


**וַיְצַו יְהוֹשֻׁעַ** בְּיּוֹם שֶׁתַּמּוּ יְמֵי בְּכִי אֵבֶל משֶׁה.


**And Yehoshua commanded.** He did this on the day that completed the days of mourning for Moshe.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ Moshe died on the seventh day of Adar and he was mourned for thirty days. See Devorim 34:8.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:11:1)


**הָכִינוּ לָכֶם צֵדָה** כָּל דָּבָר הַצָּרִיךְ לַדֶּרֶךְ וּכְלֵי זֵינָם לַמִּלְחָמָה אָמַר לָהֶם לְתַקֵּן, שֶׁאִם אַתָּה אוֹמֵר בְּמַאֲכָל וּבְמִשְׁתֶּה, הֲרֵי הָיוּ מִסְתַּפְּקִים בַּמָּן שֶׁבִּכְלֵיהֶם עַד ט"ז בְּנִיסָן, וְכֵן הוּא אוֹמֵר (לקמן ה יב): וַיִּשְׁבֹּת הַמָּן מִמָּחֳרָת (מִפִּי רַבִּי).


**Prepare your travel needs.** [Prepare] everything necessary for (traveling) and weapons for the [upcoming] battle, he said to them. You cannot say the צֵדָה refers to food and drink²⁰ maintains that while they still ate of the *manna,* they bought other varieties of food from the non-Jewish merchants, and it was these foods that they were commanded to prepare. for they had sufficient *manna* in their vessels to last them untill the sixteenth of Nissan, and so it is said:²¹ ‘The manna ceased on the morrow.’

footnotes:
²⁰ Metsudas Dovid
²¹ Chapter 5:12.


###### Rashi on Joshua 1:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:11:2)


**בְּעוֹד שְׁלשֶׁת יָמִים** בְּסוֹף שְׁלשֶׁת יָמִים, בְּעוֹד שֶׁתִּהְיוּ כָּאן שְׁלשֶׁת יָמִים וְאַחַר תַּעֲבֹרוּ.


**After three days.** At the end of three days. When you will have been here three days; then you will cross [the Jordan].



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:14:1)


**כָּל גִּבּוֹרֵי הַחַיִל** שֶׁבָּכֶם יַעַבְרוּ חֲלוּצִים.


**All who are mighty warriors** among you shall cross over armed [for battle].



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:15:1)


**מִזְרַח הַשָּׁמֶשׁ** עֵבֶר מִזְרָחִי שֶׁל יַרְדֵּן.


**The sun rises.** Refering to the eastern side of the Jordan.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Joshua 1:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/1:18:1)


**יַמְרֶה** יַקְנִיט אֶת דְּבָרֶיךָ.


**Rebels**—who challenge your words.



## Chapter 2


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:1)


**וַיִּשְׁלַח יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וְגוֹ'** עַל כָּרְחִי אֲנִי צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר שֶׁבְּתוֹךְ יְמֵי אֵבֶל משֶׁה שְׁלָחָם, שֶׁהֲרֵי לְסוֹף ג' יָמִים שֶׁתַּמּוּ יְמֵי אֵבֶל משֶׁה עָבְרוּ אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן, שֶׁמִּשָּׁם אָנוּ לְמֵדִים שֶׁמֵּת משֶׁה בְּז' אֲדָר כְּשֶׁאַתָּה מוֹנֶה ל"ג לְמַפְרֵעַ מִיּוֹם שֶׁעָלוּ מִן הַיַּרְדֵּן בֶּעָשׂוֹר לַחֹדֶשׁ הָרִאשׁוֹן, וְעַל כָּרְחָךְ מִשֶּׁנִּשְׁתַּלְּחוּ הַמְרַגְּלִים לֹא עָבְרוּ אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן עַד יוֹם הַחֲמִישִׁי, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: וַיֵּשְׁבוּ שָׁם שְׁלשֶׁת יָמִים עַד־שָׁבוּ הָרוֹדְפִים בּוֹ בַּלַּיְלָה, וַיַּעַבְרוּ וַיָּבֹאוּ אֶל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בִּן־נוּן... וַיַּשְׁכֵּם יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּבֹּקֶר וַיִּסְעוּ מֵהַשִּׁטִּים הֲרֵי יוֹם ד', וַיָּלִינוּ שָׁם טֶרֶם יַעֲבֹרוּ נִמְצָא שֶׁלֹּא עָבְרוּ עַד יוֹם ה'.


**And Yehoshua sent.** I am forced to say that it was during the mourning for Moshe that he sent them, since at the end of three days following the conclusion of the mourning for Moshe, they crossed the Jordan, and it was from this fact that we learned that Moshe died on the seventh day of Adar, by counting back thirty three days from the day they came up from the Jordan which was the tenth day of the first month.¹ And you must say that from the time the spies were sent they did not cross the Jordan until the fifth day, as it is said: they remained there three days until the pursuers returned.² That same night they crossed [the Jordan] and came back to Yehoshua ben Nun. Yehoshua rose early that morning and they departed from Shittim.³ That was the fourth day. They spent the night there before crossing. Therefore they did not cross the Jordan until the fifth day.

footnotes:
¹ The month of Nissan.
² See verse 22.
³ See Chapter 3:1.


###### Rashi on Joshua 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:2)


**חֶרֶשׁ** בְּרָז, כָּךְ תִּירְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן, אָמַר לָהֶם: עֲשׂוּ עַצְמְכֶם כְּחֵרְשִׁים, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא יַסְתִּירוּ דִּבְרֵיהֶם מִפְּנֵיכֶם. דָּבָר אַחֵר: חֶרֶשׁ חֶרֶס, הַטְעִינוּ עַצְמְכֶם קְדֵרוֹת כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּהְיוּ נִרְאִין כְּקַדָּרִין.


**Secret.** In secret—this is the translation of *Targum Yonason.* He said to them: “Pretend to be deaf-mutes so they will not conceal their activities from you.” Another explanation: translate חֶרֶשׁ as חֶרֶשׂ or חֶרֶס, [חֶרֶס means pottery [in other words] “Load yourselves with pots in order to look like pottery salesmen.”


###### Rashi on Joshua 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:3)


**רְאוּ אֶת הָאָרֶץ וְאֶת יְרִיחוֹ** וַהֲלֹא יְרִיחוֹ בִּכְלָל הָיְתָה וְלָמָּה יָצָאת, אֶלָּא שֶׁהָיְתָה קָשָׁה כְּנֶגֶד כּוּלָּם, לְפִי שֶׁהָיְתָה יוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל הַסְּפָר. וְכַיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ (שמואל ב ב ל): וַיִּפָּקְדוּ מֵעַבְדֵי דָּוִד תִּשְׁעָה־עָשָׂר אִישׁ וַעֲשָׂהאֵל, וַהֲלֹא עֲשָׂהאֵל בִּכְלָל הָיָה וְלָמָּה יָצָא, אֶלָּא שֶׁהָיָה קָשֶׁה כְּנֶגֶד כּוּלָּם, כַּיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ (מלכים א יא א): וְהַמֶּלֶךְ שְׁלֹמֹה אָהַב נָשִׁים נָכְרִיּוֹת רַבּוֹת וְאֶת בַּת פַּרְעֹה, וַהֲלֹא בַּת פַּרְעֹה בִּכְלָל הָיְתָה וְלָמָּה יָצָאת, אֶלָּא שֶׁהָיָה מְחַבְּבָהּ כְּנֶגֶד כּוּלָּם, וּכְלַפֵּי חֵטְא שֶׁהֶחֱטִיאָה אוֹתוֹ יוֹתֵר מִכּוּלָּן, כָּךְ שְׁנוּיָיה בְּסִפְרֵי (עקב נב).


**And view the land and Yericho.** Wasn’t Yericho part of the land? Why then was it mentioned specifically? To indicate that it was as strong [fortified] as the entire land combined, because it was situated on the border. There are other such verses: Among the servants of David that were missing were nineteen men and Asaheil.⁴ Wasn’t Asaheil included among the nineteen? [Why then was he mentioned specifically?] Because he was as strong as all of them. Another example: ‘And King Solomon was enamoured of many foreign wives and of Pharaoh’s daughter.’⁵ Wasn’t Pharaoh’s daughter included among them? Why then was she mentioned specifically? Because he loved her as much as all of them. And also because of the sin involved since she caused him to sin more than all of them. This was taught in Sifre.⁶

footnotes:
⁴ II Shmuel, 2:30.
⁵ I Kings, 11:1.
⁶ Bamidbar, 31:6.


###### Rashi on Joshua 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:1:4)


**אִשָּׁה זוֹנָה** תִּירְגֵּם יְהוֹנָתָן: פּוּנְדְּקִיתָא, מוֹכֶרֶת מִינֵי מְזוֹנוֹת.


**A lady innkeeper.** According to *Targum Yonasson,* an innkeeper who sold various kinds of foodstuff.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:2:1)


**לַחְפֹּר** לְרַגֵּל, כְּמוֹ (איוב לט כט): מִשָּׁם חָפַר אֹכֶל.


**To scout.** To search, and similarly it is said: ‘There he searched for food.’⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Eyov, 39:29.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:4:1)


**וַתִּצְפְּנוֹ** יֵשׁ מִקְרָאוֹת מְדַבְּרִים עַל הָרַבִּים כְּיָחִיד, לְפִי שֶׁמִּיהֲרָה בְּהַטְמָנָתָם וּבְמָקוֹם צַר, כְּאִילּוּ הָיָה יְחִידִי, וּמִדְרַשׁ אַגָּדַת תַּנְחוּמָא יֵשׁ (שלח א): פִּנְחָס וְכָלֵב הָיוּ, וּפִנְחָס עָמַד לִפְנֵיהֶם וְלֹא רָאוּהוּ לְפִי שֶׁהָיָה כְּמַלְאָךְ. דָּבָר אַחֵר: וַתִּצְפְּנוֹ כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד בִּפְנֵי עַצְמוֹ, וְדוּגְמָתוֹ מָצִינוּ (משלי כז ט): שֶׁמֶן וּקְטֹרֶת יְשַׂמַּח לֵב, וְלֹא אָמַר יְשַׂמְּחוּ לֵב.


**And she hid them.**⁸ There are scriptural verses that speak of the plural as if it were singular. Since she was in a hurry to hide them and she hid them in a narrow place⁹ it was as though they were one person. The Midrash of Rabbi Tanchuma states ‘That these two men were Pinchos and Caleiv, and Pinchos stood before them¹⁰ but they did not see him because he was [invisible] like an angel.’¹¹ Another interpretation: She hid each of them separately.¹² We find a similar verse: ‘Oil and incense gladden the heart’¹³ and it does not say יְשַׂמְחוּ לֵב, in the plural.

footnotes:
⁸ The word וַתִּצְפְנוֹ  is singular but the verse is said regarding the two men and should be plural.
⁹ She hid them in a place that normally would have sufficed for one person.
¹⁰ Before the pursuers.
¹¹ Therefore there was no need to hide him and thus she actually hid only one man.
¹² Therefore it is correct to say: she hid him.
¹³ Mishlei, 27:9.

**בְּפִשְׁתֵּי הָעֵץ** פִּשְׁתִּים בְּגִבְעוֹלֵיהֶן.


**Stalks of flax.** with their pods.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:7:1)


**עַל הַמַּעְבְּרוֹת** מְקוֹם מַעֲבַר הַמַּיִם, שֶׁהָיוּ סְבוּרִים שֶׁחָזְרוּ לַאֲחוֹרֵיהֶם אֶל עַרְבוֹת מוֹאָב, וְהַיַּרְדֵּן מַפְסִיק בֵּינְתַיִם.


**To the fords.** These were places for crossing the water,¹⁴ for the pursuers thought they had turned back to return to the plains of Moav and the Jordan was between them.

footnotes:
¹⁴ There were a number of crossing places linking both sides of the Jordan.


###### Rashi on Joshua 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:7:2)


**וְהַשַּׁעַר סָגָרוּ** הַשּׁוֹעֲרִים.


**And the shut the gate.** The gatekeepers shut the gate.¹⁵ this refers to the gate of the city. The pursuers suspected that the spies were still in the city and by shutting the gates, they would be locked in and they would be able to find them.

footnotes:
¹⁵ According to Metsudas Dovid



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:11:1)


**וְלֹא קָמָה עוֹד רוּחַ בְּאִישׁ** אֲפִילּוּ לִּשְׁכַּב עִם אִשָּׁה, אָמְרוּ: אֵין לְךָ כָּל שַׂר וְנָגִיד שֶׁלֹּא בָּא אֶל רָחָב הַזּוֹנָה, וּבַת עֶשֶׂר שָׁנִים הָיְתָה כְּשֶׁיָּצְאוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִּצְרַיִם, וְזִנְּתָה כָּל אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה (זבחים קטז ב).


**And there was no longer any spirit.** [The men had no spirit (desire)] even to lie with a woman. It was said by the Sages: There was no prince or ruler who had not had [sexual] relationships with Rochav, the harlot. She was ten years old when the Bnei Yisroel went out of Egypt, and she engaged in harlotry for forty years.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:12:1)


**אוֹת אֱמֶת** שֶׁתַּעֲשׂוּ כְּשֶׁתָּבֹאוּ וַתִּכְבְּשׁוּ אֶת הָעִיר, שֶׁתַּכִּירוּ הָאוֹת וְתַחְיוּנִי.


**A true sign.** You create the sign, so that when you come and capture the city, you will recognize the sign and keep me alive.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:15:1)


**וַתּוֹרִדֵם בַּחֶבֶל בְּעַד הַחַלּוֹן** בְּאוֹתוֹ חֶבֶל וְחַלּוֹן הָיוּ הַנּוֹאֲפִים עוֹלִין אֵלֶיהָ. אָמְרָה: רִבּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל עוֹלָם, בְּאֵלּוּ חָטָאתִי בְּאֵלּוּ תִּמְחוֹל לִי.


**Then she lowered them down with the rope.** With the same rope and through the same window her admirers would use to come up to her. She said: “Master of the World with these I have sinned, with these forgive me.”



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/2:16:1)


**עַד שׁוֹב הָרֹדְפִים** רָאֲתָה בְּרוּחַ הַקֹּדֶשׁ שֶׁיָּשׁוּבוּ לְסוֹף שְׁלשֶׁת יָמִים.


**Until the pursuers return.**¹⁶ She must have had Divine inspiration to know they would return after three days.¹⁷ maintains that she reasoned that it was a day’s journey from Yericho to the Jordan. They would spend a day searching for the spies and then return to Yericho making a total of three days.

footnotes:
¹⁶ How could she possibly have known that the pursuers would be coming back after three days?
¹⁷ Radak

**We will be free.** We are holding you responsible for this, we depend on you to make this sign.

**דָּמוֹ בְרֹאשׁוֹ** עֲוֹן הֲרִיגָתוֹ עַל רֹאשׁוֹ תְּהֵא, כִּי הוּא יִגְרוֹם מִיתָתוֹ.*


**His blood is on his own head.** The guilt of his killing is on his own head, for he will have caused his own death.

###### Rashi on Joshua 2:23:1

## Chapter 3


### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:3:1)


**וַהֲלַכְתֶּם אַחֲרָיו.** נִשְׁתַּנָּה הַמַּסָּע הַזֶּה מִשְּׁאָר מַסָּעוֹת, שֶׁכָּל זְמָן שֶׁמֹּשֶׁה הָיָה קַיָּים הָיָה עַמּוּד הֶעָנָן נוֹסֵעַ תְּחִלָּה, וּמַרְאֶה לָהֶם הַדֶּרֶךְ וְהָאָרוֹן נוֹסֵעַ אַחַר ב' דְּגָלִים, עַכְשָׁיו הָאָרוֹן נוֹסֵעַ תְּחִלָּה.


**And follow it.** This journey was different than other journeys. During Moshe’s lifetime, the Pillar of Clouds would head the procession, and show them the way, and the Ark¹ or Covenant but it is also referred to in Scripture as the אַרוֹן הָעֵדוּת, the Ark of Testimony (see Chapter 4:16) because of the Two Tablets inscribed with the Ten Commandments that were placed in the Ark. would [first begin to] move after two divisions [lit. flags]² had left, but now the Ark would travel first.

footnotes:
¹ The ark is here referred to as the Ark of the Bris
² The twelve tribes of the Bnei Yisroel were divided into four divisions or units, three tribes to a unit. When they travelled in the wilderness, two divisions of six tribes would depart the camp, to be followed by the Ark: and the other two divisions would then follow the Ark. Thus the Ark was always amidst the Bnei Yisroel.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:3:2)


**וְהַכֹּהֲנִים הַלְוִיִּם** לְפִי שֶׁיָּצְאוּ כּוּלָּם מִלֵּוִי (אֲבִי) אֲבִי עַמְרָם נִקְרְאוּ לְוִיּם, כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּרְשׁוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בִּבְרֵאשִׁית רַבָּה: בְּמ"ח מְקוֹמוֹת נִקְרְאוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים לְוִיּם. וְזֶה טַעְמוֹ שֶׁל דָּבָר (מפּי רבּי).


**The *Kohanim,* the Levites.** Since they all descended from Levi who was the father of Amram, they [the *Kohanim*] are called Levites,³ were Levites, the Levites were not *Kohanim,* because the *Kehunah* was confined to the descendants of Aharon. as our Sages explain in *Breishis Rabbah,* ‘That there are forty eight places where the *Kohanim* are referred to as Levites.’

footnotes:
³ While it is true that all Kohanim



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:1)


**אַךְ רָחוֹק יִהְיֶה** כְּבוֹדוֹ שֶׁל מָקוֹם הוּא.


**But there must be a distance.** This was mandated out of respect for Hashem.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:2)


**וּבֵינָיו** כְּמוֹ וּבֵינוֹ, וְדוֹמֶה לוֹ (תהלים קמט ב): יִשְׂמַח יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּעוֹשָׂיו. דָּבָר אַחֵר: ב' אֲרוֹנוֹת הָיוּ, שֶׁל שְׁכִינָה וְשֶׁל יוֹסֵף מְהַלְּכִין יַחַד.


**And it.** [Even though the word וּבֵינָו is read as וּבֵינָיו ] [which usually indicates the plural] nevertheless here, *it* refers to the Ark. Similarly, ‘Yisroel will rejoice in its Maker.’⁴ Another explanation: there were actually two Arks,⁵ there Ark of the Divine Presence, and the Ark containing the bones of Yosef, which travelled together.

footnotes:
⁴ Tehillim 149:2. In this verse too, the word בִעשָׂיו would seem to indicate a plural, yet it is singular, i.e., its Maker.
⁵ Accordingly the word וּבֵינָיו  correctly indicated a plural, i.e., the two Arks.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:3)


**כְּאַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה** כְּדֵי שֶׁתִּהְיוּ יְכוֹלִין לֵילֵךְ וּלְהִתְפַּלֵּל לְפָנָיו בְּשַׁבָּת, כָּךְ מְפוֹרָשׁ בְּמִדְרַשׁ תַּנְחוּמָא, לְפִי שֶׁיָּדַע שֶׁעֲתִידִין לִהְיוֹת צָרִים לִפְנֵי יְרִיחוֹ בַּשַּׁבָּת.


**Two thousand Amos.** To permit them to go and to pray before the Ark on Shabbos.⁶ was the limit permitted for a Jew to walk outside his domain on Shabbos. This is the explanation of Midrash Tanchumah, for Yehoshua knew that eventually they would beseige Yericho on Shabbos.

footnotes:
⁶ Two thousand Amos


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:4)


**לְמַעַן אֲשֶׁר תֵּדְעוּ** מוּסָב עַל: וַהֲלַכְתֶּם אַחֲרָיו.


**So that you will know**⁷ This refers to the last words of verse 3, “and follow it.”

footnotes:
⁷ Certainly this cannot be the reason why they were commanded them to keep a distance from the Ark.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:4:5)


**כִּי לֹא עֲבַרְתֶּם בַּדֶּרֶךְ** לֹא הֲלַכְתֶּם בְּעִנְיָן זֶה עַד הֵנָּה.


**For you have never travelled this route.** You have never travelled in this fashion until now.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ** בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁלִישִׁי, וְיוֹם ל' שֶׁל אֵבֶל הָיָה רִאשׁוֹן לְג' הַיָּמִים.


**And Yehoshua said.** This was on the third day, and the thirtieth day of mourning [for Moshe] was on the first of the three days.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:2)


**הִתְקַדָּשׁוּ** הִזְדַּמְּנוּ.


הִתְקַדָשׁוּ usually means sanctify yourselves, but here it means prepare yourselves.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:3)


**כִּי מָחָר** בְּעָבְרְכֶם אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן.


**For tomorrow** when you cross the Jordan.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:5:4)


**נִפְלָאוֹת** תִּירְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: פְּרִישָׁן.


**Wonders.** This translation follows that of *Targum Yonasan.*



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:6:1)


**וַיֹּאמֶר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אֶל הַכֹּהֲנִים** בְּיוֹם הַמָּחֳרָת.


**And Yehoshua said to the Kohanim.** He said this on the next day.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:6:2)


**שְׂאוּ אֶת אֲרוֹן הַבְּרִית** עַד עַכְשָׁיו נָשְׂאוּ הַלְוִיִּם, וְהַיּוֹם הַכֹּהֲנִים.


**Carry the Ark of the Covenant.** Until now it was carried by the Levites, but today it was to be carried by the *Kohanim.*



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:8:1)


**כְּבֹאֲכֶם עַד קְצֵה מֵי הַיַּרְדֵּן** כְּשֶׁתִּכַּנְסוּ לִפְנִים מִשְּׂפָתוֹ.


**When you reach the edge of the waters of the Jordan.** When you enter within its edge.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:8:2)


**בַּיַּרְדֵּן תַּעֲמֹדוּ** עַד אֲשֶׁר יַעַבְרוּ כָּל הָעָם אֶל הָעֵבֶר הַשֵּׁנִי.


**Remain standing in the Jordan.** Until all the people cross over to the other side [of the river.]



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:9:1)


**גְּשׁוּ הֵנָּה** צִמְצֵם אֶת כּוּלָּם בֵּין שְׁנֵי בַּדֵּי אָרוֹן, זֶה אֶחָד מִן הַמְּקוֹמוֹת שֶׁהֶחֱזִיק מוּעָט אֶת הַמְרוּבֶּה.


**Move closer.** He compressed all of them between the two poles of the Ark. This is one of the places where a small space was able to hold⁸ a large amount [of people.]

footnotes:
⁸ This was obviously a Divine miracle.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:10:1)


**בְּזֹאת תֵּדְעוּן** בְּמַה שֶּׁאַתֶּם רוֹאִים שֶׁכֻּלְּכֶם מְצוּמְצָמִין כָּאן.


**With this you will know.** By virtue of actually seeing that all of you are compressed here.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:11:1)


**הַבְּרִית אֲדוֹן כָּל הָאָרֶץ** בְּרִית שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא, שֶׁהוּא אֲדוֹן כָּל הָאָרֶץ. וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ עַל הַהֵ"א שֶׁל הַבְּרִית, שֶׁהַרְבֵּה מִקְרָאוֹת מְדַבְּרִים כָּךְ, כְּמוֹ: (מלכים ב טז יז) הַמִּסְגְּרוֹת הַמְּכוֹנֹת: (ירמיהו לא לט) הָעֵמֶק הַפְּגָרִים.


**The covenant of the Master of all the earth.** The covenant of the Holy One, blessed is He, Who is the Master of all the earth. Do not wonder about the letter ה of the word הַבְּרִית⁹ for there are many Scriptual words that are so constructed, such as: ‘הַמִסְגְּרוֹת הַמְכוֹנוֹת, the borders of the foundation.’¹⁰ הָעֵמֶק הַפְּגָרִים, the valley of the corpses.¹¹

footnotes:
⁹ It should have been written בְּרִית אָדוֹן, without the ה.
¹⁰ II Malachim 16:17. It should have been written מִסְגְרוֹת הַמְכוֹנוֹת without the ה'.
¹¹ Yirmeyahu 31:39. It should have been written עֵמֶק הַפְגָרִים without the ה.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:11:2)


**עֹבֵר לִפְנֵיכֶם** מַקְדִּים לִפְנֵיכֶם לִיכָּנֵס לְתוֹךְ הַיַּרְדֵּן.


**Will cross over before You.** It will go before you to enter into the Jordan.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:12:1)


**קְחוּ לָכֶם שְׁנֵי עָשָׂר אִישׁ** הָכִינוּ אוֹתָם לִהְיוֹת נְכוֹנִים לְמַה שֶּׁאֲצַוֶּה אוֹתָם כְּשֶׁתַּעַבְרוּ אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן.


**Take for yourselves twelve men.** Prepare them to be available to fulfil that which I will command them when you finally cross the Jordan.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:13:1)


**הַמַּיִם הַיֹּרְדִים מִלְמָעְלָה** לְמַטָּה, כְּדֶרֶךְ כָּל הַנְּהָרוֹת הַמּוֹשְׁכִים.


**The waters that descend from above.** Coming down from the heights which is the way of all flowing rivers.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:13:2)


**וְיַעַמְדוּ נֵד אֶחָד** כְּשֶׁיֵּרְדוּ הַמַּיִם עַד מָקוֹם שֶׁנִּכְרְתוּ שָׁם וְאֵינָם יוֹרְדִים מִשָּׁם וּלְמַטָּה, אֶלָּא הֵם מִתְקַבְּצִים וְנִגְדָּשִׁין וְעוֹלִין לְמַעְלָה, כִּיפִּין עַל גַּבֵּי כִּיפִּין.


**And will stand in one massive mound.** As the water flows down to the place where it will be cut off, it will be unable to flow further downstream, but will be gathered and piled up, and grow higher, layer upon layer.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:13:3)


**נֵד** כְּמוֹ (תהלים לג ז): כּוֹנֵס כַּנֵּד: (ישעיהו יז יא), נֵד קָצִיר, לְשׁוֹן גּוֹבַהּ, וְכֵן חִבְּרָם מְנַחֵם.*


**Mound.** Similar to: ‘He gathers like a mound,’¹² ‘a mound of harvest.’¹³ These are expressions of height, and so did Menachem connect them.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹² Tehillim 33:7.
¹³ Yeshayahu 17:11.
¹⁴ Menachem was an authority on Grammar often quoted by Rashi.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:15:1)


**גְּדוֹתָיו** לְשׁוֹן שָׂפָה גְּבֹהָה.


**Its banks.** This is an expression of a high edge.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:15:2)


**כָּל יְמֵי קָצִיר** שֶׁיְּמֵי נִיסָן יְמֵי קָצִיר הֵם.


**During the entire harvest time.** The days of Nissan coincide with the harvest.¹⁵ says this increase of the flood level of the Jordan naturally increased the magnitude of the miracle of its waters being cut off.

footnotes:
¹⁵ The crossing of the Jordan took place during the month of Nissan. Metsudas Dovid



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:1)


**הַרְחֵק מְאֹד** מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁעָמְדוּ שָׁם נִפְסְקוּ.


**A great distance** from the place they stood. There the waters were cut off.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:2)


**מֵאָדָם הָעִיר** כָּךְ שְׁמָהּ.


**From Odom.** [Adom] was the name of the city.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:3)


**וְהַיֹּרְדִים** אוֹתָם שֶׁהָיוּ יוֹרְדִים מִשָּׁם וּלְמַטָּה תְּחִלָּה.


**Descended.** Those waters that had descended from above that had previously flowed downstream.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:16:4)


**תַּמּוּ נִכְרָתוּ** עָבְרוּ כְּדֶרֶךְ הֲלִיכָתָן כְּכָל הַנְּחָלִים הַהוֹלְכִים אֶל הַיָּם עַד אֲשֶׁר תַּמּוּ.


**Were completely cut off.** They continued flowing on their course¹⁶ as do all the streams that flow into the sea, which ended their course.

footnotes:
¹⁶ This refers to those waters that had flowed toward the Salt Sea before the cut off of the upper waters.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Joshua 3:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:17:1)


**וַיַּעַמְדוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים** אֵצֶל שְׂפַת הַיַּרְדֵּן.


**And the Kohanim remained standing.** They remained standing at the edge of the Jordan.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:17:2)


**בְּתוֹךְ הַיַּרְדֵּן** וְכָל זְמָן שֶׁעָמְדוּ לֹא יָרְדוּ הַמַּיִם הָעֶלְיוֹנִים לְמַטָּה, וּבְתוֹךְ כָּךְ כָּל הָעָם עוֹבְרִים בְּחָרָבָה.


**In the midst of the Jordan.** So long as they remained standing, the upper waters did not descend below, and during this time all the people were crossing on dry land.


###### Rashi on Joshua 3:17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/3:17:3)


**הָכֵן** מְכוּוָּנִים וְנִצָּבִים זֶה כְּנֶגֶד זֶה.


**Firmly** They were placed firmly, exactly opposite each other.



## Chapter 4


### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:2:1)


**שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר אֲנָשִׁים** הֵם שֶׁנִּזְכָּרִים לְמַעְלָה לִהְיוֹת נְכוֹנִים.


**Twelve men.** These were the men mentioned previously,¹ who were told to be prepared.

footnotes:
¹ See Rashi 3:12.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:3:1)


**וְהַעֲבַרְתֶּם אוֹתָם עִמָּכֶם** כְּמִצְוַת משֶׁה (דברים כז ד) לִבְנוֹת לָהֶם מִזְבֵּחַ בְּהַר עֵיבָל וְלִכְתּוֹב עֲלֵיהֶם אֶת דִּבְרֵי הַתּוֹרָה, וּבוֹ בַּיּוֹם בָּאוּ אֶל הַר עֵיבָל וּבָנוּ בָּהֶם אֶת הַמִּזְבֵּחַ וְהֶעֱלוּ עוֹלוֹת וּשְׁלָמִים, וְאָכְלוּ וְשָׁתוּ, וְקִפְּלוּ אוֹתָם וּבָאוּ וְלָנוּ בַּגִּלְגָּל.


**And cross them over with you.** To fulfil the command of Moshe to build with them an Altar on Mt. Eivel, and to write upon them the words of Torah. On that same day they came to Mt. Eivel and built with them the Altar. They offered up burnt-offerings and peace-offerings, and they ate and drank. They then scraped the stones, and came and lodged in Gilgal.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:5:1)


**עִבְרוּ לִפְנֵי אֲרוֹן וְגוֹ'** הִכָּנְסוּ עַתָּה בַּיַּרְדֵּן וְעִבְרוּ עַד לִפְנֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים.


**Pass before the Ark.** Enter into the Jordan now and cross over until you are in front of the *Kohanim.*



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:7:1)


**וְהָיוּ הָאֲבָנִים הָאֵלֶּה** בַּגִּלְגָּל לְזִכְרוֹן זֶה.


**And these stones will be** in Gilgal as a commemoration of this.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:9:1)


**וּשְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה אֲבָנִים** אֲחֵרוֹת הֵקִים יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בְּתוֹךְ הַיַּרְדֵּן.


**Twelve stones.** These were different stones² that Yehoshua set up in the midst of the Jordan.

footnotes:
² Obviously these were not the stones mentioned in the previous verse.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:10:1)


**עַד תֹּם כָּל־הַדָּבָר** כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בְּמַסֶּכֶת סוֹטָה (לד א): עוֹדָם בַּיַּרְדֵּן, אָמַר לָהֶם יְהוֹשֻׁעַ: דְּעוּ לָמָּה אַתֶּם עוֹבְרִים אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן, עַל מְנָת שֶׁתּוֹרִישׁוּ אֶת כָּל יוֹשְׁבֵי הָאָרֶץ וְכוּ'.


**Until the the completion of everything.** This is as we learn in *Masches Sotah,* 34a: While they were standing in the Jordan, Yehoshua said to them, “You must know the purpose for which you are crossing the Jordan. It is on the condition that you drive away all the inhabitants of the land.”



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:11:1)


**וַיְהִי כַּאֲשֶׁר תַּם כָּל הָעָם לַעֲבוֹר וַיַּעֲבֹר אֲרוֹן וְגוֹ'** לֹא כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁעָבְרוּ הָאֲחֵרִים, אֶלָּא כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפוֹרָשׁ לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן (להלן פסוק יח): נִתְּקוּ כַּפּוֹת רַגְלֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים לַאֲחוֹרֵיהֶם אֶל הֶחָרָבָה עַל הַשָּׂפָה שֶׁנִּכְנְסוּ בָּהּ, שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹמְדִים אֶצְלָהּ, וְחָזְרוּ הַמַּיִם לַאֲחוֹרֵיהֶם לֵילֵךְ כִּתְמוֹל שִׁלְשׁוֹם; נִמְצָא, אֲרוֹן וְנוֹשְׂאָיו מִצַּד זֶה וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל מִצַּד זֶה, נָשָׂא הָאֲרוֹן אֶת נוֹשְׂאָיו וְעָבַר.


**And it was when all the people had finished crossing and the Ark crossed.** The Ark did not cross as the others did, but in the manner explained below:³ ‘The soles of the feet of the *Kohanim* were lifted and they were moved back to the dry land, on the bank from which they initially entered. They were standing next to it and the waters were returned to their course to flow as they did before. The result of all this was that the Ark and its bearers were on this side and all the Bnei Yisroel were on the other (lit. this) side. The Ark then lifted its bearers and crossed over.’

footnotes:
³ Verse 18.


###### Rashi on Joshua 4:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:11:2)


**לִפְנֵי הָעָם** לְעֵינֵי הָעָם.


**In the people’s presence.** Before the eyes of the people.



### Verse 16

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:16:1)


**צַוֵּה אֶת הַכֹּהֲנִים** כָּאן פֵּירַשׁ הַכָּתוּב אֶת הָאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה, הֵיאָךְ עָבַר אֲרוֹן ה' וְהַכֹּהֲנִים לִפְנֵי הָעָם וְגוֹ'.


**Command the *Kohanim.*** Here is where Scripture explains what was mentioned before⁴ how the Ark of Adonoy and the *Kohanim* crossed in the people’s presence.

footnotes:
⁴ Verse 11.


###### Rashi on Joshua 4:16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:16:2)


**וְיַעֲלוּ מִן הַיַּרְדֵּן** 'וְיַעַבְרוּ' אֵין כְּתִיב כָּאן, אֶלָּא 'וְיַעֲלוּ', לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁעַל הַשָּׂפָה שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹמְדִין אֶצְלָהּ עָלוּ, וְאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר שֶׁהָיוּ עוֹמְדִין אֵצֶל שְׂפַת הַיַּרְדֵּן שֶׁבַּמַּעֲרָב, שֶׁהֲרֵי נֶאֱמַר לְמַעְלָה (ג טו) נִטְבְּלוּ רַגְלֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים בִּקְצֵה הַמַּיִם, וְשָׁם נֶאֱמַר וַיַּעַמְדוּ הַכֹּהֲנִים וְכָל יִשְׂרָאֵל עוֹבְרִים.


**Have them come up out of the Jordan.** It is not written, ‘have them cross’ but rather ‘have them come up.’ From this we learn that from the bank upon which they were standing did they come up. And it is impossible to claim that they were standing beside the western bank of the Jordan, for it is said previously, ‘that the feet of the *Kohanim* were immersed in the edge of the water.’⁵ And there it is said, ‘And the *Kohanim* remained standing— and all the Bnei Yisroel crossed over.’⁶ remained standing at the point of entrance, they were on the Eastern bank of the Jordan.

footnotes:
⁵ Chapter 3:15.
⁶ Ibid 16. Obviously since the Bnei Yisroel crossed the Jordan from East to West, and the Kohanim



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:18:1)


**נִתְּקוּ כַּפּוֹת רַגְלֵי הַכֹּהֲנִים** מִן הַמַּיִם אֶל הֶחָרָבָה שֶׁאֶצְלָם, וַיָּשׁוּבוּ הַמַּיִם לִמְקוֹמָם; נִמְצָא, אֲרוֹן מִצַּד זֶה וְיִשְׂרָאֵל מִצַּד זֶה, נָשָׂא אֲרוֹן אֶת נוֹשְׂאָיו וְעָבַר. וְעַל דָּבָר זֶה נֶעֱנַשׁ עוּזָּא (שמואל ב ו ז) כְּשֶׁאָחַז בָּאָרוֹן, נוֹשְׂאָיו נָשָׂא, עַצְמוֹ לֹא כָּל שֶׁכֵּן.


**The soles of the feet of the *Kohanim* were lifted.** [They were lifted] from the water to the dry land next to them, and the water returned to their place. The result of this was that the Ark was on this [eastern] side and the Bnei Yisroel were on this [the western] side. Thus the Ark lifted its carriers and crossed over. It was regarding this that Uzzah was punished ‘when he took hold of the Ark,’⁷ for if the Ark carried its carriers, it certainly was able to carry itself.⁸

footnotes:
⁷ II Shmuel, VI: 6,7.
⁸ Thus by taking hold of the Ark, Uzzah manifested his fear that the Ark was going to fall off the wagon.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Joshua 4:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/4:20:1)


**הֵקִים יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בַּגִּלְגָּל** הוּא הַמָּלוֹן אֲשֶׁר לָנוּ בּוֹ הַלַּיְלָה.


**Yehoshua set up in Gilgal.** This was the lodging place where they spent that night.



## Chapter 5


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:1:1)


**אֲשֶׁר בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן יָמָּה** לְצַד שֶׁעָבְרוּ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל, הוּא צַד שֶׁל מַעֲרָבִי, וְעַד עַכְשָׁיו הָיוּ בְּעֵבֶר מִזְרָחִי.


**Who were on the western side of the Jordan.** On the side to which Bnei Yisroel crossed, which is the western side, since they had been on the eastern side.



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:2:1)


**חַרְבוֹת צֻרִים** כְּתַּרְגּוּמוֹ: אִזְמְלָוָן חֲרִיפִין, וְכֵן (תהלים פט מד): אַף תָּשִׁיב צוּר חַרְבּוֹ, כְּשֶׁהַחֲרִיפוּת נֶהְפָּךְ לַצְּדָדִין וְאֵינוֹ חוֹתֵךְ יָפֶה, וְכֵן (ישעיהו נד יז): כָּל כְּלִי יוּצַר עָלַיִךְ.


**Sharp knives.** As translated by Targum, sharp knives. Similarly, ‘You also turned back the sharp edge of his sword.’¹ When the sharp edge is turned sideways it does not cut well. ‘Similarly, ‘Every weapon that is sharpened against you?’²

footnotes:
¹ Tehillim 89:44.
² Yeshayahu 54.


###### Rashi on Joshua 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:2:2)


**שֵׁנִית** שֶׁמָּלוּ כְּבָר בְּלֵיל יְצִיאָתָם מִמִּצְרַיִם קָהָל גָּדוֹל יַחַד, וְזוֹ פַּעַם שֵׁנִית, שֶׁכָּל הָאַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה שֶׁהָיוּ בַּמִּדְבָּר לֹא נָשְׁבָה לָהֶם רוּחַ צְפוֹנִית וְלֹא הָיָה לָהֶם יוֹם נוֹחַ לָמוּל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁשָּׁנִינוּ בִּיבָמוֹת (עא ב). וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (שם): שֵׁנִית זוֹ פְּרִיעַת מִילָה שֶׁלֹּא נִתְּנָה לְאַבְרָהָם אָבִינוּ.


**The second time.** For there had already been a circumcision— on the night of their departure from Egypt a large assembly were collectively circumcised, and this was the second time; because during the entire forty years that they were in the wilderness the north wind did not blow for them, and they did not have a suitable day to circumcise themselves, as we learned in *Maseches Yevamos.*³ Our Sages said, “The second time refers to exposing the corona at circumcision. [This particular aspect of circumcision] was not commanded to our Patriarch, Avrohom.

footnotes:
³ Yevomos 71b. Certain atmospheric conditions were beneficial for the healing of the wound caused by circumcision. The north wind created this condition. These winds were withheld during the forty years in the wilderners in order that the “Clouds of Glory” that led and protected the people would not disperse.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:3:1)


**גִּבְעַת הָעֲרָלוֹת** עַל שֵׁם הַמְּאוֹרָע נִקְרָא, שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה כְּמִין גִּבְעָה.


**Gival Ha-orlos.** [Lit. Hill of the foreskins] It was so called, because of the event, [i.e., the mass of foreskins that were circumcised] which were made into a hill.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:4:1)


**וְזֶה הַדָּבָר** עַל יְדֵי דִּבּוּר מָל אוֹתָם, אָמַר לָהֶם: סְבוּרִים אַתֶּם לִירַשׁ אֶת הָאָרֶץ עֲרֵלִים, לֹא כָּךְ נֶאֱמַר לְאַבְרָהָם (בראשית יז ט): וְאַתָּה אֶת־בְּרִיתִי תִשְׁמֹר וְגוֹמֵר (שם שם ח), וְנָתַתִּי לְךָ וּלְזַרְעֲךָ אַחֲרֶיךָ אֵת אֶרֶץ מְגֻרֶיךָ.


**And this is the reason.** [Lit. and this is the thing] After this speech he circumcised them. He said to them. “Do you think you will inherit this land while you are yet uncircumcised? Was it not said to Avrohom, ‘And you will preserve My covenant etc.’ ‘and I will give you and your descendants after you, the land of your sojournings.’”⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Bereishis 17:8,9. Thus the inheritance of the land was dependent on their preserving the covenant, which was the rite of circumcision.


###### Rashi on Joshua 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:4:2)


**כָּל הָעָם הַיּוֹצֵא** לֹא הָיָה אֶחָד מֵהֶם כָּאן, שֶׁכֻּלָּם מֵתוּ, וְהֵם הָיוּ מוּלִים, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר: כִּי מֻלִים הָיוּ.


**All the people that went out.** Not one of them was here for they had all died, and they had all been circumcised, as it is said, ‘for they were circumcised.’⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Next verse.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:5:1)


**לֹא מָלוּ** כְּמוֹ שֶׁפֵּירַשְׁתִּי, שֶׁלֹּא נָשְׁבָה רוּחַ צְפוֹנִית, וְהֵם שֶׁמָּלוּ עַכְשָׁיו.


**Were not circumcised.** As I have previously explained, the north wind had not blown.⁶ It was these who were now to be circumcised.

⁶ See Rashi to verse 2.



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:7:1)


**וְאֶת בְּנֵיהֶם הֵקִים תַּחְתָּם** וְאֶת בְּנֵיהֶם שֶׁהֵקִים תַּחְתֵּיהֶם, הֵם הַיִּלּוֹדִים בַּמִּדְבָּר, אוֹתָם מָל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ.


**And their children whom He brought up in place of them.** And their children whom He brought up in place of them, they were born in the wilderness it was them whom Yehoshua circumcised.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:8:1)


**וַיֵּשְׁבוּ תַּחְתָּם** (תַּרְגּוּם:) בַּאֲתַרְהוֹן, וְלֹא צָרוּ עַל הָעִיר.


**They remained in their places.** They remained in their places and they did not besiege the city.


###### Rashi on Joshua 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:8:2)


**עַד חֲיוֹתָם** מִן הַמַּכָּה.


**Until they were healed** from their wounds.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:9:1)


**גַּלּוֹתִי** הֲסִירוֹתִי אֶת חֶרְפַּת מִצְרַיִם, שֶׁהָיוּ אוֹמְרִים: רְאוּ כִּי רָעָה נֶגֶד פְּנֵיכֶם, כּוֹכָב אֶחָד יֵשׁ שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ 'רָעָה' וְהוּא סִימָן שֶׁל דָּם, רוֹאִין אָנוּ אוֹתוֹ עֲלֵיכֶם בַּמִּדְבָּר, וְהוּא שֶׁאָמַר משֶׁה בַּמִּדְבָּר (שמות לב יב): לָמָּה יֹאמְרוּ מִצְרַיִם לֵאמֹר בְּרָעָה הוֹצִיאָם, וְהֵם אֵינָם יוֹדְעִים שֶׁהוּא דַּם מִילָה, וּכְשֶׁמָּלוּ בִּימֵי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ וּבָא אוֹתוֹ הַדָּם הוּסְרָה אוֹתָהּ הַחֶרְפָּה שֶׁעֲדַיִין עֵרֶב רַב שֶׁעָלוּ עִמָּהֶם הָיוּ מוֹנִים לָהֶם, כָּךְ דָרַשׁ רַבִּי משֶׁה הַדַּרְשָׁן.


**I have removed.** (Lit. I have rolled) I have removed the ridicule of the Egyptians who had said [when they were leaving], “See that ‘*Raah*’ is before you.⁷ There is a star named *Raah,* which is a symbol of blood. We see it overpowering you in the wilderness.”⁸ This is what Moshe referred to in the wilderness [when he said,] “Why should the Egptians speak, saying, ‘With *Raah,* He took them out.’” But they did not know that [the blood they saw] was the blood of circumcision. And now when they were circumcised in the days of Yehoshua, and the blood of circumcision appeared, that ridicule was removed; for the mixed multitude that went up with them [from Egypt] were still taunting them. This is the way R’ Moshe, Ha Darshan explained [this passage.]

footnotes:
⁷ Shemos, 10:10.
⁸ The Egyptians intimated that Bnei Yisroel would meet with disaster if they left Egypt because they would be under the power of the star of blood.


###### Rashi on Joshua 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:9:2)


**גַּלּוֹתִי** הֲסִירוֹתִי, כְּמוֹ: (תהלים קיט כב), גַּל מֵעָלַי חֶרְפָּה: (בראשית כט י), וַיָּגֶל אֶת הָאֶבֶן.


**I have removed.** [Lit. I have rolled] This is similar to: ‘Remove from me humiliation and contempt;’⁹ ‘and he rolled off the stone.’¹⁰

footnotes:
⁹ Tehillim 119:22.
¹⁰ Bereishis 29:10.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:11:1)


**מִמָּחֳרַת הַפֶּסַח** יוֹם הֲנָפַת הָעוֹמֶר, שֶׁהִקְרִיבוּ עוֹמֶר תְּחִלָּה וּמִז' בַּאֲדָר שֶׁמֵּת משֶׁה, שֶׁפָּסַק הַמָּן, הָיוּ מִסְתַפְּקִין עַד עַכְשָׁיו מִמָּן שֶׁבִּכְלֵיהֶם שֶׁלָּקְטוּ בְּשִׁבְעָה בַּאֲדָר, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמות טז לה): אֶת הַמָּן אָכְלוּ אַרְבָּעִים שָׁנָה, וַהֲלֹא מ' שָׁנָה חָסֵר שְׁלשִׁים יוֹם, שֶׁהֲרֵי תְּחִלַּת יְרִידַת הַמָּן בְּט"ו בְּאִיָיר, אֱמוֹר מֵעַתָּה, בַּחֲרָרָה שֶׁהוֹצִיאוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִמִּצְרַיִם טָעֲמוּ טַעַם מָן.


**From the day after the Pesach.** This was the day of waving the *Omer,* for they sacrificed the *Omer* first.¹¹ From the seventh day of Adar, the day when Moshe died, when the *manna* stopped falling, they were sufficiently supplied with the *manna* which was in their vessels which they gathered on the seventh of Adar, as it is said, ‘They ate the *manna* for forty years.’¹² But these forty years lacked thirty days— for the *manna* began to fall on the fifteenth day of Iyar.¹³ We must say therefore that in the [matzo] cakes the Bnei Yisroel took with them from Egypt, they tasted the taste of *manna.*

footnotes:
¹¹ They could not have eaten from the new grain of Eretz Yisroel until after they brought the Omer sacrifice. See Vayikroh 23:14.
¹² Shemos 16:35.
¹³ Shemos 161, 13. See Rashi’s comments there.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:12:1)


**וְלֹא הָיָה עוֹד לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל מָן** לְפִיכָךְ: וַיֹּאכְלוּ מֵעֲבוּר הָאָרֶץ, אִם הָיָה לָהֶם מָן לֹא הָיוּ אוֹכְלִים מִן הַתְּבוּאָה, שֶׁהַמָּן הָיָה נוֹחַ לָהֶם, מָשָׁל אוֹמֵר לַתִּינוֹק: מִפְּנֵי מָה אַתָּה אוֹכֵל פַּת שְׂעוֹרִין, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין לִי פַּת חִטִּין, לְכָךְ נֶאֱמַר: וְלֹא הָיָה עוֹד וְגוֹ'.


**No longer did the Bnei Yisroel have *manna*.** Therefore they ate of the grain of the land. If they would have had *manna* they would not have eaten of the grain, for the *manna* was agreeable to them. There is a parable for this: One says to a child, “Why are you eating barley bread?” “Because I have no wheat bread.” That is why it is said, ‘no longer did etc.’



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:13:1)


**בִּהְיוֹת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בִּירִיחוֹ** מִכָּאן לְעִיבּוּרָהּ שֶׁל עִיר שֶׁהִיא מִן הָעִיר, שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר לוֹמַר בְּתוֹךְ יְרִיחוֹ.*


**When Yehoshua was in Yericho.** From here we can learn that the outskirts of a city are considered part of the city itself, for it impossible to say that Yoshua was actually inside Yericho.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ Since the Bnei Yisroel had not yet captured Yericho, he could not have been in the city itself.


###### Rashi on Joshua 5:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:13:2)


**הֲלָנוּ אַתָּה** (תַּרְגּוּם:) הַלְמִסְעַדְנָא אֲתֵיתָא.


**Are you with us?** Have you come to support us?



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:14:1)


**עַתָּה בָאתִי** לְעֶזְרָתְךָ, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם יָכוֹל לְהִלָּחֵם עָלֶיהָ וּלְתוֹפְשָׂהּ לְהַפִּיל הַחוֹמָה, אֲבָל בִּימֵי משֶׁה רַבָּךְ בָּאתִי וְלֹא חָפֵץ בִּי, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שמות לג טו): אִם אֵין פָּנֶיךָ הֹלְכִים וְגוֹ'.


**I have now come.**—“to help you, for it is impossible for man to battle against it [Yericho] and capture it, to knock down the wall. However in the days of your master, Moshe, I came, but he did not want my presence, as it is said, ‘If your Presence [Adonoy] does not go, etc.’”¹⁵

footnotes:
¹⁵ Shemos 33:15.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Joshua 5:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/5:15:1)


**שַׂר צְבָא ה'** יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁהוּא צָבָא לַה', וּמִיכָאֵל הָיָה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דניאל י כא): מִיכָאֵל שַׂרְכֶם.


**The officer of Adonoy’s host.** This refers to Bnei Yisroel, who are Adonoy’s host. The officer mentioned here is the Angel, Michoeil, as it is said, ‘Michoeil, your officer.’¹⁶

footnotes:
¹⁶ Daniel 10:21.

**Shut and barred.** [Lit. closed and closed] Targum translates: ‘Closed with iron gates and reinforced with bars of copper.’

**A long blast.** When the final blast is sounded, and the blower extends the sound.

### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:9:1)


**וְהֶחָלוּץ** בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן וּבְנֵי גָד הָיוּ עוֹבְרִים לִפְנֵיהֶם, לְפִי שֶׁהָיוּ בְּנֵי גָד גִּבּוֹרִים וּמַכִּים בִּזְרוֹעַ חֲזָקָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דברים לג כ): וְטָרַף זְרוֹעַ אַף קָדְקֹד.


**And the warriors.** These were the men of the tribes of Reuvein and Gad who went before them, because the men of Gad were mighty warriors and they would smite with a strong arm, as it is said, ‘And he rips off the shoulder and the head.’¹

footnotes:
¹ Devorim 33:20.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Joshua 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:13:1)


**וְהַמְאַסֵּף** שֵׁבֶט דָּן הַנּוֹסֵעַ אַחֲרוֹן, וְהוּא מְאַסֵּף אֶת כָּל הַמִּתְעַכְּבִים הָאַחֲרוֹנִים.


**The rearmost.** This refers to the tribe of Dan that traveled last and gathered all those who lagged behind.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Joshua 6:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:15:1)


**בַּיּוֹם הַשְּׁבִיעִי** שַׁבָּת הָיָה.


**On the seventh day.** This was on the Shabbos.²

² They were permitted to wage war and to kill on Shabbos by the Divine command, that instructed them to lay siege to the city according to the word of God, as told to Yehoshua. See verses 2-5, this chapter.



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Joshua 6:17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:17:1)


**וְהָיְתָה הָעִיר חֵרֶם** הֶקְדֵּשׁ, כִּי הַיּוֹם שַׁבַּת קֹדֶשׁ, וְרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת קֹדֶשׁ שָׁלָל הַנִּשְׁלָל בּוֹ.


**And the city shall be consecrated.** It shall be sacred, for that day was the holy Shabbos,³ maintains that Yehoshua issued this edict by Divine ordination, but also mentions that the Sages were of the opinon that Yehoshua issued this edict on his own. and it was therefore fitting that all the booty that was taken be holy.

footnotes:
³ See Rashi to verse 15. Radak



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Joshua 6:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:18:1)


**וַעֲכַרְתֶּם** לְשׁוֹן מַיִם עֲכוּרִים.


**And bring disturbance.** This is an expression of disturbed waters.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Joshua 6:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:23:1)


**וַיָּבֹאוּ הַנְּעָרִים הַמְרַגְּלִים** כָּאן הָיוּ צְרִיכִים זֵירוּז וְנַעֲשׂוּ כִּנְעָרִים זְרִיזִים, וּבַלַּיְלָה הָרִאשׁוֹן הָיוּ כְּמַלְאָכִים שֶׁשָּׁמְרוּ עַצְמָן מִן הָעֲבֵירָה עִם רָחָב הַזּוֹנָה, לְכָךְ נִקְרְאוּ שָׁם מַלְאָכִים, וּלְכָךְ נִקְרְאוּ: אֲנָשִׁים, מַלְאָכִים, נְעָרִים.


**And the lads who were the spies went in.**⁴ This mission required speed, thus they performed like speedy lads. However on the first night⁵ they were like angels, guarding themselves from sinning with Rochov, the harlot, therefore they were referred to as angels. Therefore they were referred to as men, as angels and as lads.

footnotes:
⁴ In verse 22 they are referred to as men. In verse 17 they are referred to as מַלְאָכִים (Lit angels) and here they are referred to as lads. Rashi seeks to explain why they were described in these terms.
⁵ This refers to the night they arrived at the house of Rochov at the beginning of their mission.



### Verse 26

###### Rashi on Joshua 6:26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/6:26:1)


**בִּבְכֹרוֹ יְיַסְּדֶנָּה וּבִצְעִירוֹ** בִּתְחִלַּת יְסוֹד שֶׁיִּבְנֶה בָּהּ יָמוּת בְּנוֹ הַבְּכוֹר, וְיִקְבְּרֶנוּ וְיֵלֵךְ עַד שֶׁיָּמוּת הַצָּעִיר בִּגְמַר הַמְּלָאכָה, הִיא הַצָּבַת הַדְּלָתוֹת.


**[The death of] his first born etc.** [the death of] his youngest son etc. When he begins laying the foundation upon which he is building it [the city], his first born son will die and he will bury him, and he will continue doing so⁶ until the youngest son dies at the completion of the work which is the setting of its portals.

footnotes:
⁶ He will continue burning his sons.
