**מִן הַלָּשֹׁן** שֶׁל יָם.


**From the bay**—of the sea.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:1)


**וְיָצָא אֶל מִנֶּגֶב לְמַעֲלֵה עַקְרַבִּים** וְאֵינוֹ אוֹמֵר 'וְיָצָא וְנָסַב וְתָאַר', אֶלָּא בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁחוּט שֶׁל גְּבוּל בּוֹלֵט אֶל הַחוּץ, אוֹ כּוֹנֵס לְצַד פְּנִים שֶׁאֵינוֹ הוֹלֵךְ מְכֻוָּון, וְכָאן הוּא בּוֹלֵט אֶל הַחוּץ וּבָא מִנֶּגֶב לְמַעֲלֵה עַקְרַבִּים בַּדָּרוֹם שֶׁל מַעֲלֵה עַקְרַבִּים, נִמְצָא שֶׁמַּעֲלֵה עַקְרַבִּים לִפְנִים מִן הַחוּט, וְעָבַר וּבָא לוֹ אֶל הַמַּעֲרָב.


**And it extended southward to Maalei-Akrabim.** The words: וְיָצָא, and it extended, וְנַסֵּב, and it encircled, and וְתָּאַר, and it encompassed, are used only in the place where the boundary line bulges outward, or is indented inward, and does not follow a straight line, and here it bulges outward and reaches to the south of Maalei-Akrabim, in the south of Maalei Akrabim. Thus Maalei-Akrabim is within the line, and [the line] passed it and continued westward,


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:2)


**צִנָה** לְצִן, וְכָל תֵּיבָה שֶׁצְּרִיכָה לַמֶ"ד בִּתְחִלָּתָהּ הִטִּיל לָהּ הֵ"א בְּסוֹפָהּ.


**To Tzin.** (צִנָה) is the same as לְצִין , to Tzin. Every word that requires a *lamed* as, a prefix will have a *hei* as a suffix.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:3)


**וְעָלָה** כָּל מַה שֶּׁהוּא עוֹלֶה מִן הַמִּזְרָח, לְצַד יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הוּא עוֹלֶה, וּמִיְּרוּשָׁלַיִם וָהָלְאָה הוּא יוֹרֵד. כָּאן לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁיְּרוּשָׁלַיִם גְּבוֹהָה מִכָּל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. וִירוּשָׁלַיִם אֵינָהּ נִזְכֶּרֶת בְּמֶצֶר זֶה, שֶׁבְּמֶצֶר צְפוֹנִית שֶׁל יְהוּדָה הָיְתָה, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר בָּעִנְיָן.


**And went up.** Whatever rises eastward towards *Yerushalayim* goes up higher; and from Yerushalayim and further it descends. From this we learn that Yerushalayim is higher than any place in Eretz Yisroel. Yerushalayim is actually not mentioned in the boundary since it was in the northern boundary of Yehudah³ as is stated later in this chapter.⁴

footnotes:
³ These verses discuss the southern boundary.
⁴ See below verse 8.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:4)


**וְעָלָה מִנֶּגֶב לְקָדֵשׁ בַּרְנֵעַ** הַחוּט הוֹלֵךְ לִדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל קָדֵשׁ בַּרְנֵעַ, נִמְצֵאת קָדֵשׁ לִפְנִים מִן הַחוּט.


**And went up from the south to Kodeish-barneia.** The line went from the south of Kodeish-barneiah; thus, Kodeish is within the [boundary] line.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:3:5)


**וְעָבַר חֶצְרוֹן** לְצַד הַמַּעֲרָב הוּא מוֹנֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ, עַד: וְהָיוּ תֹּצְאֹתָיו הַיָּמָּה.


**And it passed Chetzron.** He continues counting (the cities) westward until “and its boundary extended to the sea.”⁵

footnotes:
⁵ See next verse.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:4:1)


**תֹּצְאוֹת** סוֹפוֹ שֶׁל מֶצֶר אֶל הַיָּם הַגָּדוֹל, שֶׁהוּא מֶצֶר מַעֲרָבִי לְכָל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. נִמְצָא גְּבוּל יְהוּדָה מַחֲזִיק כָּל אָרְכָּהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב, וְעוֹמֵד בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל.


**Extended.** The end of the border was at the Great Sea,⁶ which is the western border of all of Eretz Yisroel. Thus the boundary of Yehudah occupies the entire length of Eretz Yisroel from east to west and is located in the south of Eretz Yisroel.

footnotes:
⁶ The Mediterranean Sea.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:1)


**וּגְבוּל קֵדְמָה** חוּט הַמָּתוּחַ לְמֶצֶר מִזְרָחִי שֶׁל יְהוּדָה.


**The eastern boundary.** Referring to the boundary line that stretches along the eastern border of Yehudah.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:2)


**יַם הַמֶּלַח** שֶׁהוּא מִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית לָאָרֶץ בְּ'אֵלֶּה מַסְעֵי' (במדבר שם).


**The Salt (Dead) Sea.** It is located at the south eastern corner of the land, as stated in Parshas אֵלֶה מַסְעֵי⁷

footnotes:
⁷ Bamidbar 34:12.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:3)


**עַד קְצֵה הַיַּרְדֵּן** רָחְבּוֹ שֶׁל גְּבוּל יְהוּדָה אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא כְּנֶגֶד יָם הַמֶּלַח, עַד מָקוֹם שֶׁהַיַּרְדֵּן נוֹפֵל בְּיַם הַמֶּלַח, שֶׁהַיַּרְדֵּן אַף הוּא בְּמֶצֶר מִזְרָח שֶׁל אֶרֶץ כְּנָעַן, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּ'אֵלֶּה מַסְעֵי' בְּמֶצֶר מִזְרָחִי (שם לד יב): וְיָרַד הַגְּבוּל הַיַּרְדֵּנָה וְהָיוּ תּוֹצְאֹתָיו יָם הַמֶּלַח, שֶׁהוּא בְּמִקְצוֹעַ.


**To the end of the Jordan.** The width of the boundary of Yehudah is only that which is opposite the Dead Sea to the place where the Jordan empties into the Dead Sea, for the Jordan itself is also on the eastern border of the land of Canaan, as stated in Parshas אֵלֶה מַסְעֵי⁸ regarding the eastern border. The boundary descended to the Jordan and it extended to the Dead Sea, which is at the corner.

footnotes:
⁸ Ibid.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:5:4)


**וּגְבוּל לִפְאַת צָפוֹנָה** חוּט מֶצֶר הַמָּתוּחַ לְמֶצֶר צָפוֹן מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב, מִלְּשׁוֹן יַם הַמֶּלַח מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁהַיַּרְדֵּן נוֹפֵל בּוֹ, שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ שֶׁשָּׁם כָּלָה רוֹחַב גְּבוּל מֶצֶר הַמִּזְרָחִי וְעָלָה לְצַד הַמַּעֲרָב בֵּית חָגְלָה, וְעָבַר הַחוּט מִצָּפוֹן לְבֵית הָעֲרָבָה, נִמְצָא בֵּית הָעֲרָבָה בְּתוֹךְ גְּבוּל יְהוּדָה לִפְנִים מִן הַחוּט.


**And the boundary of the north side.** The border line stretching along the northern border from east to west goes from the bay of the Dead Sea, from the place where the Jordan empties into it, as we have said that the width of the eastern boundary ends there; and it goes up westward to Beis Chogloh, and then the boundary line passes to the north of Beis HaArovoh which is the south of Eretz Yisroel. Beis HaArovo therefore is included in the boundary of Yehudah within the boundary line.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:6:1)


**וְעָלָה הַגְּבוּל אֶבֶן בֹּהַן** כָּל מַה שֶּׁהוֹלֵךְ עַד יְרוּשָׁלַיִם הוּא עוֹלֶה.


**The boundary went up to Even bohan.** As the border line goes toward Yerushalayim, it becomes elevated⁹ the city was called *Even,* and the ruler of the city was a man by the name of Bohan, son of Reuvein. According to *Metzudos Tzion,* the city itself was called *Even Bohan,* and its ruler was a man called ben Reuvein.

footnotes:
⁹ See Rashi to Verse 3. There is a difference of opinion regarding the name of the the place. According to Radak,



### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:1)


**וְעָלָה הַגְּבוּל דְּבִרָה מֵעֵמֶק עָכוֹר** שֶׁהָיָה עֵמֶק עָכוֹר בֵּין אֶבֶן בֹּהַן לַדְּבִיר.


**The boundary went up to Devir from the Valley of Ochor.** The Valley of Ochor was between Even Bohan and Devir.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:2)


**וְצָפוֹנָה פֹּנֶה אֶל הַגִּלְגָּל** וּכְשֶׁמַּגִּיעַ כְּנֶגֶד הַגִּלְגָּל, מַרְחִיב הַגְּבוּל וְיוֹצֵא חוּט הַמֶּצֶר לְצַד הַצָּפוֹן אֶל הַגִּלְגָּל, אֲשֶׁר הוּא נוֹכַח מַעֲלֵה אֲדֻמִּים אֲשֶׁר הַמַּעֲלֶה בִּדְרוֹם הַנַּחַל, נִמְצָא הַנַּחַל חוּץ מִן הַחוּט שֶׁלֹּא בִּגְבוּל יְהוּדָה.


**And it turned northward to Gilgol.** When it reached Gilgol, the boundary widens, and the borderline extends northward to Gilgol which is opposite Maalei-adumim, whose elevation [Maalei] is south of the Valley. The valley therefore is outside the borderline and is not within the boundary of Yehudah.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:3)


**מִנֶּגֶב לַנַּחַל** עֵמֶק וּמִדְרוֹן הָיָה בְּלֹא מַיִם.


**South of the Valley.** It was a steep incline without water.¹⁰

footnotes:
¹⁰ נַחַל is usually translated as a river, but since as Rashi comments, there was no water, he translates it as a valley.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:7:4)


**אֶל עֵין רֹגֵל** מַעְיַן הַכּוֹבְסִים, תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: עֵין קַצְרָא, וְקוֹרֵא אֶת הַכּוֹבֵס רוֹגֵל, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁבּוֹעֵט אֶת בִּגְדֵי הַצֶּמֶר בְּרַגְלָיו, פולוש בְּלַעַ"ז (דוֹרֵךְ־בְרַגְלָיו).


**To Ein-rogel** A spring where laundry was washed, which is translated by Targum Yonason, עֵין קַצְרָה. In Scripture a launderer is called רגֵל because he treads woolen clothes with his feet, (רֶגֶל). folos in old French.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:8:1)


**וְעָלָה וְגו' גֵּי בֶן הִנֹּם** עוֹדֶנּוּ עוֹלֶה מְעַט עַד עֵין עֵיטָם וּמִשָּׁם וָהָלְאָה הוּא יוֹרֵד, וְזֶהוּ שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ ז"ל בִּשְׁחִיטַת קָדָשִׁים (זבחים נד ב): סָבוּר לְמִבְנָיְיהוּ בְּעֵין עֵיטָם שֶׁהוּא גָּבוֹהַּ מִירוּשָׁלַיִם מְעַט.


**The boundary went up to the valley of Ben-hinom.** It continued going up somewhat until it reached Ein-itim and from there on it descends. This is what our Rabbis, of blessed memory, said in the chapter of שְׁחִיטַת קָדְשִׁים:¹¹ They considered building [the Beis Ha Mikdosh] in Ein-itim which is a bit higher than Yerushalayim.¹² higher than *Yerushalayim*, but they said, “There is nothing more pleasing about the ox than his shoulders.

footnotes:
¹¹ See Maseches Zevachem 54b.
¹² Since the verse states, “וּבֵין כְּתֵפָיו שָׁכֵן, and He resides between his shoulders,” (Devorim 33:12), they remarked, “Let us descend a little, because the shoulders are below the head.” The verse states that the Almighty will reside between the shoulders of the boundary of Binyomin, i.e., that the Beis Ha Mikdosh which is the resting place of the Shechina, will be built in the area of the Tribe of Binyomin. King David thought it should be built at Ein-itim which is twenty-three  amohs


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:8:2)


**אֶל כֶּתֶף הַיְבוּסִי מִנֶּגֶב** נִמְצֵאת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם חוּץ מִן הַחוּט וְאֵינָהּ בִּגְבוּל יְהוּדָה אֶלָּא בִּגְבוּל בִּנְיָמִין שֶׁהוּא בִּצְפוֹנוֹ שֶׁל יְהוּדָה.


**To the southern side of the Yevusi.** Yerushalayim is outside the boundary line, and is not in the boundary of Yehudah, but within the boundary of Binyomin which is to the north of Yehudah.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:9:1)


**וְתָאַר** לְשׁוֹן וְנָסַב בְּעוֹגֶל, כְּמוֹ (ישעיהו מד יג): וּבַמְּחוּגָה יְתָאֳרֵהוּ, וְיוֹנָתָן תִּירְגֵּם אֶת כֻּלָּם 'וְיִסְחֹר'.


**And it encompassed.** This is an expression of circling, similar to, “and with a compass, he circles it.”¹³ Targum Yonasan translates them all, and he encircled.

footnotes:
¹³ Yeshayahu 44:13.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:9:2)


**וְיָצָא אֶל עָרֵי הַר עֶפְרוֹן** הַחוּט יוֹצֵא לְצַד הַצָּפוֹן, וְהַגְּבוּל הִרְחִיב עַד עָרֵי הַר עֶפְרוֹן.


**And extended to the cities of Mount Efron.** The boundary line extended northward, and the boundary widens to encompass the cities of Mount Efron.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:9:3)


**וְתָאַר הַגְּבוּל בַּעֲלָה** אֵין זֶה לְצַד הַמַּעֲרָב, אֶלָּא הַבְּלִיטָה לְצַד צָפוֹן.


**And the boundary circled to Baaloh.** This is not in a westerly direction, but its projection was to the north.



### Verse 10

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:10:1)


**וְנָסַב הַגְּבוּל מִבַּעֲלָה יָמָּה** עַכְשָׁיו חוֹזֵר לְמִנְיָנוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹן, מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב.


**The boundary turned from Baaloh westward.** Now he reverts to his previous description [of the cities] from the east to the west.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:10:2)


**אֶל כֶּתֶף הַר יְעָרִים מִצָּפוֹנָה** שֶׁהָיָה הַחוּט בִּצְפוֹנָהּ שֶׁל הַר יְעָרִים, נִמְצָא הַר יְעָרִים בְּתוֹךְ גְּבוּל יְהוּדָה לִפְנִים מִן הַחוּט.


**To the side of Mount Yearim to the north.** The borderline was to the north of Mount Yearim. Thus Mount Yearim was within the boundary of Yehudah, within the borderline.



### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:11:1)


**וְהָיוּ תֹּצְאוֹת הַגְּבוּל** סוֹף הַמֶּצֶר.


**The boundary went out**— Referring to the end of the boundary.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:11:2)


**יָמָּה** אֶל יָם הַגָּדוֹל שֶׁהוּא מִצַּד מַעֲרָבִי.


**Toward the sea.** Referring to the Great Sea [the Mediteranean] which is the western boundary [of Eretz Yisroel].



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:13:1)


**אֶל פִּי ה' לִיהוֹשֻׁעַ** כְּמָה שֶׁאָמַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לִיהוֹשֻׁעַ.


**According to the word of Adonoy to Yehoshua.** As the Holy one, blessed is He, said to Yehoshua.¹⁴

footnotes:
¹⁴ See above 14:9,10. Even though the city of Chevron had been promised to Caleiv (see Rashi to Bamidbor 14:24), it was not clear within the territory of which tribe it would be located. It was Divinely ordained that Chevron would fall within the territory of the tribe of Yehudah, so that Caleiv would not be separated from his fellow tribesmen.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:14:1)


**וַיֹרֶשׁ מִשָּׁם כָּלֵב** לְאַחַר מִיתַת יְהוֹשֻׁעַ, כִּי עֲדַיִין בִּימֵי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לֹא נִלְכְּדָה חֶבְרוֹן, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר שׁוֹפְטִים (א כ), וְלֹא נִכְתְּבָה כָּאן אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי הַחֲלֻקָּה.


**And Caleiv drove out from there.** This happened after the death of Yehoshua, for during the days of Yehoshua Chevron had not yet been captured, as it is said in the book of *Shoftim.*¹⁵ It was recorded here only because of its relationship to the division of the land [among the tribes.]

footnotes:
¹⁵ See Shoftim 1:20.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:15:1)


**קִרְיַת סֵפֶר** רַבּוֹתֵינוּ אָמְרוּ (תמורה טז א): אֵלּוּ הֲלָכוֹת שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּכְּחוּ בִּימֵי אֶבְלוֹ שֶׁל משֶׁה שֶׁהֶחֱזִיר עָתְנִיאֵל בֵּן קְנַז מִפִּלְפּוּלוֹ.


**Kiryas Seifer.** Our Rabbis said, “These are the laws that were forgotten during the days of mourning for Moshe, which Osniel, son of Kenaz, rediscovered through his brilliant dialectic scholarship.¹⁶ of Torah, and challenged them to rediscover the forgotten laws. He announced that the one who would overcome, וּלְכָדָה (conquer) these problems would be worthy to become his son-in-law.

footnotes:
¹⁶ The Hebrew name for Devir was Kiryas Seifer, which means the city of the Book. The Talmud, Maseches, Temurah 16A, relates the following: Rav Yehudah said in the name of Rav, “When Moshe was about to leave this world, he said to Yehoshua, ‘Do you have any questions regarding the laws of the Torah? Ask me about them and I will teach them to you now.’ Yehoshua replied, ‘Rebbe! Did I leave your presence even for an hour? Did I ever travel to another place? Did you yourself not write about me in the Torah, “But his servant Yehoshua, son of Nun, a young man never departed from the Tabernacle.’” (Shemos 33:11) Thus Yehoshua implied that since he served Moshe faithfully for forty years and had studied Torah diligently, there was nothing that he did not know. This was considered a sin for Yehoshua and as a result, he immediately forgot three hundred laws, and seven hundred additional laws became unclear to him. During the month of mourning for Moshe, the Bnei Yisroel forgot many more laws. A total of three thousand laws were forgotten during this period. Rav Avahu said, “Nevertheless they were brought back by Osniel, son of Kenaz through his dialectic scholarship, as it is said, “And Osniel, son of Kenaz, conquered it (Kiryas Seifer).” After Caleiv conquered Chevron he approached the scholars in the Beis Midrash who were called יוֹשְׁבֵי דְבִיר, those who sit in Devir, because they sat all day studying דִבְרֵי תוֹרָה, the words



### Verse 17

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:17:1

**אֲחִי כָלֵב** מֵאִמּוֹ.


**He [Osniel] was the brother of Caleiv.** He was his brother from his mother.¹⁷

¹⁷ After Caleiv’s father, Yefuneh, died, his mother married Kenaz and she gave birth to Osniel.



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:18:1)


**וַתִּצְנַח** (תַּרְגּוּם:) וְאִתְרְכִינַת, הִטְּתָה עַצְמָהּ לִיפּוֹל בְּרַגְלָיו.


**She leaned [off the donkey].** According to Targum, וְאִתְרְכִינַת, she leaned. She bent herself to fall at his feet.¹⁸

footnotes:
¹⁸ According to the Talmud, Maseches Temurah, 16A, Achsoh, rode to her wedding on a donkey to hint to her father that just as a donkey begins to bray when there is no food in his stall, so a woman complains when there is no food in the house. When she saw that her father did not take the hint, she fell off the donkey at his feet. Caleiv then realized that something was wrong.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:19:1)


**תְּנָה לִי בְרָכָה** פַּרְנָסָה.


**Give me a blessing.** Give me sustenance.¹⁹

footnotes:
¹⁹ In asking her father for a blessing, she asked for a portion of land from which she and her husband could support themselves.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:19:2)


**אֶרֶץ הַנֶּגֶב** חֲרֵבָה, כְּמוֹ (בראשית ח יג): חָרְבוּ פְּנֵי הָאֲדָמָה, וּמְתַרְגֵּם: נְגִיבוּ, שייקא בְּלַעַ"ז (יְבֵשָׁה), בַּיִת מְנֻגָּב מִכָּל טוּב, אָדָם שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ אֶלָּא תּוֹרָה.


**Dry land.** חַרֵבָה —dry,²⁰ land. Rashi therefore explains why it can be translated also as a dry land. which is similar to “The surface of the ground had dried,”²¹ and there Onkelos translates חָרְבוּ as נְגִיבוּ. ²² In French it is rendered sec. [Achsoh said to Caleiv, “You gave me dry land”] I.e., a house that is “dry” of all good, a man who desires only Torah.²³

footnotes:
²⁰ The verse states  אֶרֶץ הַנֶגֶב, which would usually be translated as a southern
²¹ See Bereishis 8:13.
²² Thus the word נֶגֶב  also implies חֲרֵבָה which means dry. The southern portion of Eretz Yisroel is referred to as the נֶגֶב, because it is the driest part of the land.
²³ Achsoh actually complimented her husband, Osniel, who was totally dedicated to God and Torah, and had no desire to enjoy the pleasures of this world. See Maseches Temurah, 16A.


###### Rashi on Joshua 15:19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/15:19:3)


**נְתַתָּנִי** נָתַתָּ לִי, כְּמוֹ (שם לז ד): דַּבְּרוֹ לְשָׁלוֹם—דַּבֵּר אֵלָיו; (ירמיהו י כ): בָּנַי יְצָאֻנִי—יָצְאוּ מִמֶּנִּי; (מלכים א יט כא): בִשְּׁלָם־בִּשֵּׁל לָהֶם.


**You have given me.** The word נְתַתָּנִי is the same as נָתַתָּ לִי, as in: (the preposition לִי is missing and seems to be like a direct object. Rashi will offer similar examples of this structure.) They could not speak speak to him peacefully.²⁴ ‘My children have gone away from me.’²⁵ ‘He cooked for them.’²⁶

²⁶ See I Melachim 19:21. There too the word בִשְׁלָם is the same as בִשֵׁל לָהֶם.

**וַיִּהְיוּ הֶעָרִים מִקְצֵה לְמַטֵּה בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה** בִּגְבוּל מַטֵּה יְהוּדָה, הוּא שֶׁאָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ (גיטין ז א): לֹא מָנָה יְהוֹשֻׁעַ אֶלָּא עֲיָירוֹת הָעוֹמְדוֹת עַל הַגְּבוּלִים.


**The most remote cities of the tribe of the descendants of Yehudah.** Referring to the border of the tribe of Yehudah. This is what our Sages stated, “Yehoshua only counted the towns that were on the borders.²⁷

footnotes:
²⁷ Obviously there were many more cities within the borders. These cities are mentioned to show the courage and bravery of the tribe of Yehudah. They were not afraid to live near the border of Edom, even though there was always the danger that the Edomites would attack them.

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:32:1

**כָּל עָרִים עֶשְׂרִים וָתֵשַׁע** וּבִפְרָטָן אַתָּה מוֹצֵא שְׁלשִׁים וּשְׁמוֹנֶה, אֶלָּא אֵלֶּה הַיְּתֵרוֹת נָטְלוּ בְּנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן מֵחֶבֶל בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה, וְנִזְכָּרוֹת הֵם בְּנַחֲלַת שִׁמְעוֹן: בְּאֵר שֶׁבַע, וּמוֹלָדָה, חֲצַר שׁוּעָל, וָעָצֶם, וְאֶלְתּוֹלַד, וְחָרְמָה, וְצִקְלַג, וְעַיִן וְרִמּוֹן הֲרֵי תֵּשַׁע הַיְּתֵרוֹת הַמְּנוּיוֹת כָּאן.


**Altogether there were twenty nine cities.** If you count them however you will find that there were thirty eight. These are the extra nine cities counted here.

###### Rashi on Joshua 15:63:1

**לֹא יָכְלוּ בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה לְהוֹרִישָׁם** שָׁנִינוּ בְּסִפְרֵי: רַבִּי יְהוֹשֻׁעַ בֶּן קָרְחָה אוֹמֵר: יְכוֹלִין הָיוּ, אֶלָּא שֶׁלֹּא הָיוּ רַשָּׁאִין מֵחֲמַת הַשְּׁבוּעָה שֶׁנִּשְׁבַּע אַבְרָהָם לַאֲבִימֶלֶךְ, וִיבוּסִי זֶה לֹא עַל שֵׁם הָאוּמָּה הָיָה, אֶלָּא מִגְדַּל דָּוִד שֶׁהָיָה בִּירוּשָׁלַיִם וּשְׁמוֹ יְבוּס, וּבְנֵי אוֹתוֹ מָחוֹז מִפְּלִשְׁתִּים הָיוּ, וּכְשֶׁכָּבְשׁוּ בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה אֶת יְרוּשָׁלַיִם לֹא הוֹרִישׁוּ אֶת בְּנֵי אוֹתוֹ מָחוֹז.


**The descendants of Yehudah were unable to expel them.** We learned in Sifri: “Rabbi Yehoshua be Korcho says, ‘They were really able [to expel them] but they were not permitted to do so because of the oath that Avrohom had sworn to Avimelech.³¹ These Yevusim Were not part of the Yevusi nation,³² but the Tower of David which was in *Yerushalayim* was named Yevus, and those who lived in that vicinity were of the Philishtim. Thus when the descendants of Yehudah conquered Yerushalayim, they did not expel the inhabitants of that vicinity.

footnotes:
³¹ See Bereishis 21:23. These Yevusim were descendants of Avimelech, whom Avrohom had sworn not to harm. Avimelech witnessed all the miracles that God performed on Avrohom’s behalf, and he asked him to swear that he would not harm him or his descendants for three generations and Avrohom agreed.
³² Why then were they called Yevusim? Rashi goes on to explain that they were actually of the Philishtim and thus protected by Avrohom’s oath.



## Chapter 16


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Joshua 16:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:1:1)


**וַיֵּצֵא הַגּוֹרָל לִבְנֵי יוֹסֵף** בְּמֶצֶר צְפוֹנִי שֶׁל גְּמַר כִּיבּוּשׁוֹ שֶׁל יְהוֹשֻׁעַ נָפַל לָהֶם הַגּוֹרָל, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְּמַטָּה: וּבְנֵי יוֹסֵף יַעַמְדוּ עַל גְּבוּלָם מִצָּפוֹן, וְהַרְבֵּה מִן הָאָרֶץ הָיָה בֵּין בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה וּבֵין בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף, שֶׁנָּטְלוּ שְׁאָר הַשְּׁבָטִים אַחֲרֵיהֶם (רָצָה לוֹמַר, בֵּינֵיהֶם), כְּמוֹ שֶׁמָּצִינוּ בְּשֵׁבֶט בִּנְיָמִין (לקמן יח יא): וַיֵּצֵא גְּבוּל גּוֹרָלָם בֵּין בְּנֵי יְהוּדָה וּבֵין בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף.


**The lot for the descendants of Yoseif came out** at the northern border which marked the end of Yehoshua’s conquest. There, the lot fell for them, as stated below, “And the house of Yoseif will remain in their boundary on the north.”¹ Much of the land [of Eretz Yisroel] lay between [the border of] the descendants of Yehudah, [and the border of] the descendants of Yoseif. The other tribes took their inheritance from this area, as we find below regarding the tribe of Binyomin, “And the boundary of their lot was between the descendants of Yehudah [to the south] and the descendants of Yoseif [to the north.]

footnotes:
¹ See chapter 18:5.


###### Rashi on Joshua 16:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:1:2)


**מִיַּרְדֵּן יְרִיחוֹ לְמֵי יְרִיחוֹ מִזְרָחָה** הִתְחִיל הַגְּבוּל לַמִּזְרָח.


**From the Jordan at Yericho to the waters of Yericho to the east.** This is where the eastern border begins.


###### Rashi on Joshua 16:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:1:3)


**הַמִּדְבָּר עוֹלֶה מִירִיחוֹ** אֶל הַמִּדְבָּר עוֹלֶה הַגְּבוּל מִירִיחוֹ וּבָא לוֹ לְצַד מַעֲרָב לְבֵית אֵל.


**To the desert that goes up from Yericho.** The boundary goes up from Yericho to the desert and comes westward to Beis-eil.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 16:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:5:1)


**מִזְרָחָה עַטְרוֹת אַדָּר** לְמִזְרָח מַתְחִיל מֵעַטְרוֹת אַדָּר, וּמִשָּׁם רוֹחַב תְּחוּמוֹ עַד בֵּית חוֹרוֹן הֲרֵי רָחְבּוֹ.


**On the east was Atros-addor.** The eastern boundary starts from Atros-Addor and from there the border widens to Beis-choron. This is its width.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Joshua 16:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:6:1)


**וְיָצָא הַגְּבוּל הַיָּמָּה** אָרְכּוֹ הָיְתָה לְצַד הַמַּעֲרָב אֶל הַמִּכְמְתָת, מִצָּפוֹן שֶׁל הַמִּכְמְתָת הוֹלֵךְ הַחוּט.


**The boundary then went out westward.** Its length was westward to Michmesos; the boundary line continued north of Michmesos.


###### Rashi on Joshua 16:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:6:2)


**וְנָסַב הַגְּבוּל** מִתְפַּשֵּׁט רוֹחַב הַתְּחוּם לְצַד צָפוֹן, וְהַחוּט יוֹצֵא בִּבְלִיטָתוֹ בְּמִזְרָחָהּ שֶׁל תַּאֲנַת שִׁילֹה.


**The boundary turned.** The width of the border spread northward, and the border-line bulged outward to the east of Taanis-Shiloh.²

footnotes:
² See chapter 18:11. Even though in verse 4, Menashe is mentioned before Ephraim, the portion of Ephraim is described first in verse 5. Verse 4 mentions them in the order of their birth and Menashe was the oldest, but when Yaakov blessed them, he placed Ephraim first, because he knew that Ephraim would be the greater of the two. Therefore when the lots were drawn, the lot of Ephraim came out first.


###### Rashi on Joshua 16:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:6:3)


**וְעָבַר אוֹתוֹ** וְעָבַר הַגְּבוּל אֶת תַּאֲנַת שִׁילֹה, וְעָבַר הַחוּט מִמִּזְרָח שֶׁל יָנוֹחַ כָּל זֶה בְּלִיטַת הָרוֹחַב, עַד 'וְיָצָא הַיַּרְדֵּן', וְשָׁם עִיר וּשְׁמָהּ תַּפּוּחַ, וּמִשָּׁם יִסּוֹב הַחוּט לְמִדַּת אוֹרֶךְ הַגְּבוּל יָמָּה לְנַחַל קָנָה.


**And passed by it.** The boundary passed by Taanis-shiloh,³ were permitted to be sacrificed and eaten in Shiloh, such as: *Shelamin,* (peace-offerings) *Todah,* (thanksgiving-offerings) the *korban Pesach,* (the paschal lamb) and the first-born offerings. Various tithes (maasir) could also be brought to the Mishkan at Shiloh. After the Mishkan at Shiloh was destroyed, the people mourned as they would at the destruction of the Beis Hamikdosh in Yerushalayim. Whenever they passed by its ruins they wood weep and mourn. It was therefore known as Taanis-Shiloh, which implies the mourning of Shiloh. and the boundary line passed east of Yonoach. This was all included in the bulge of the width, which terminated at the Jordan, and there was a city named Tapuach. From there the boundary line turned to the measure of the length of the westward boundary to the brook of Konnoh

footnotes:
³ This refers to Shiloh, where the Mishkan was to be built. This new Mishkan was built with walls of stone and was more permanent than the Mishkan that Moshe built with wooden walls. This Mishkan remained standing in Shiloh for 369 years. In many ways it was similar to the Beis HaMikdash that was built in Yerushalayim. Many of the korbonas

###### Rashi on Joshua 16:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/16:9:1)


**וְהֶעָרִים הַמִּבְדָּלוֹת** וְגַם זֶה עוֹד מִנַּחֲלַת אֶפְרַיִם, עָרִים הָיוּ לָהֶם מוּבְדָּלוֹת מִתְּחוּמוֹ וּמֻבְלָעוֹת בְּתוֹךְ נַחֲלַת בְּנֵי מְנַשֶּׁה.


**The separated cities.** These too were included in the inheritance of Ephraim. They had cities that were separated from their portion and were incorporated within the boundary of the inheritance of Menashe.⁴

## Chapter 17


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Joshua 17:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:1:1)


**לְמָכִיר בְּכוֹר מְנַשֶּׁה** לְפִיכָךְ נָטַל תְּחִלָּה בִּימֵי משֶׁה בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן, וַיְהִי לוֹ הַגִּלְעָד וְהַבָּשָׁן.


**To Mochir, the firstborn of Menashe.** Since he was the firstborn, he was the first to take a portion in the days of Moshe, on the other side of the Jordan, and thus Gilod and Boshon were his portion.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 17:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:5:1)


**וַיִּפְּלוּ חֶבְלֵי מְנַשֶּׁה עֲשָׂרָה** שִׁשָּׁה לְשִׁשָּׁה בָּתֵּי אָבוֹת הַמְּנוּיִים לְמַעְלָה, וְאַרְבַּע לִבְנוֹת צְלָפְחָד, לֹא שֶׁתְּהֵא כָּל אַחַת בֵּית אָב לְעַצְמָהּ אֶלָּא שֶׁנָּטְלוּ אַרְבָּעָה חֲלָקִים, חֵלֶק שֶׁל אֲבִיהֶם שֶׁהָיָה מִיּוֹצְאֵי מִצְרַיִם, וּלְפִי מִנְיַן הַיּוֹצְאִים מִמִּצְרַיִם נִתְחַלְּקָה הָאָרֶץ וְחִלְּקוּ עִם אֶחָיו בְּנִכְסֵי חֵפֶר אָבִיו, שֶׁהָיָה גַּם הוּא מִיּוֹצְאֵי מִצְרַיִם וְשֶׁהָיָה בְּכוֹר וְנָטַל שְׁנֵי חֲלָקִים, וְעוֹד הָיָה לָהֶם חֵלֶק אֲחִי אֲבִיהֶם שֶׁמֵּת בַּמִּדְבָּר בְּלֹא בָּנִים, וְנָטְלוּ נַחֲלָה בְּחֶלְקוֹ, כָּךְ אָמְרוּ רַבּוֹתֵינוּ בְּבָּבָא בַּתְרָא (קיח א), וְלֹא הוּזְקַק הַכָּתוּב לְהַשְׁמִיעֵנוּ מִנְיַן חֶלְקֵי הַבָּנוֹת, אֶלָּא לְלַמֵּד שֶׁנָּטְלוּ חֵלֶק בְּכוֹרָה וּלְהוֹדִיעַ שֶׁאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל חֵלֶק יְרֻשָּׁה לָהֶם מֻחְזֶקֶת מֵאֲבוֹתֵיהֶם, שֶׁאִילּוּלֵי כֵּן אֵין הַבְּכוֹר נוֹטֵל בָּרָאוּי כְּבַמּוּחְזָק.


**Ten portions of land fell to Menashe.** Six portions went to the family divisions mentioned above, and four went to the daughters of Tselophchod, not because each of them was considered an individual family division but rather because they took *these* four portions: the portion of their father who was one of those who went out of Egypt; since according to the number of those who went out of Egypt was the land divided; the portion their father shared with his brothers in the property of their father, Cheifer, since he too was one of those who went out of Egypt; and since their father, Tselophchod, was the firstborn, he received two portions [of Cheifer’s property]; and an additional portion from their father’s brother who died in the wilderness, without children and they received the inheritance of his share. This was stated by our Sages in *Bava Basra.*¹ It was unnecessary for Scripture to relate to us the number of the daughters’ share, except to teach us that they took the share of the firstborn, and to inform us that Eretz Yisroel was an actual inheritance to them [since it was considered] the actual possession of their fathers, for the firstborn does not receive an extra portion in potential property as he does in actual property.²

footnotes:
¹ Maseches Bava Basra 119A.
² A firstborn receives a double portion only of the property that actually belonged to his father, but not of the property that was only potentially his father’s. Regarding the inheritance of Eretz Yisroel, the portions that were destined for them were considered their actual possession and not just potential property. The daughters of Tzelophechod, therefore, were able to inherit that extra portion of their father.

### Verse 11

###### Rashi on Joshua 17:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:11:1)


**שְׁלשֶׁת הַנָּפֶת** (תַּרְגּוּם:) תְּלָתָא פְּלָכִין, וּ'שְׁלשֶׁת הַנָּפֶת' הַנִּזְכָּרִים כָּאן שֶׁל דֹּאר וְשֶׁל עֵין דּוֹר הָיוּ, כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַעְלָה בְּסֵפֶר זֶה (יב כג): מֶלֶךְ דּוֹר לְנָפַת דּוֹר, וּבְפָסוּק אַחֵר אוֹמֵר (לעיל יא ב): לְנָפֶת דֹּאר מִיָּם, וְכָאן כָּךְ הוּא אוֹמֵר: וְאֶת יוֹשְׁבֵי דֹּאר וּבְנוֹתֶיהָ שְׁלשֶׁת הַנָּפֶת שֶׁהָיוּ לָהּ.


**These were three regions.** [as the Targum], Three provinces. The three regions mentioned here consisted of Dor and Ein dor, as stated above in this Seifer:³ “The king of Dor of the Dor region,” and in another verse it states:⁴ “In the region of Dor on the west,” and here it is stated: “The inhabitants of Dor and its towns.” These are three regions that belonged to her.

³ Above 12:23.

### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Joshua 17:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:12:1)


**וַיּוֹאֶל** וַיֹּאבֶה.


**Resolved**—were willing.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ The tribe of Menashe set a bad precedent by taxing the Canaanites instead of destroying them as the Torah commanded. This evil practice was soon adopted by the other tribes. Even though the tribe of Ephraim also did not drive out the Canaanites (see above 16:10), they at least forced them into slave labor, whereas the tribe of Menashe demanded only a money tax. This was considered a graver sin as it allowed these Canaanites the ability to influence the Jews much more than those who were actually enslaved.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Joshua 17:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:14:1)


**וַיְדַבְּרוּ בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף** שֵׁבֶט מְנַשֶּׁה.


**And the descendants of Yoseif spoke.** Referring specifically to the descendants of Menashe.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ According to Rashi even though the tribe of Ephraim was part of the “descendants of Yoseif”, they did not join the tribe of Menashe in their complaint. The reason being, as Rashi later explains, was that while the tribe of Menashe had experienced a sharp increase of their population, the tribe of Ephraim experienced a decrease in its population, and were therefore quite satisfied with their portion of inheritance. This is based on the opinion that the size of the individual tribe’s inheritance was determined by their number at the time they left Egypt. During the years in the desert Menashe grew faster than any other tribe. When they left Egypt their number was 32,200 and when they entered Eretz Yisroel it was 52,700, an increase of 20,500. They were given land for 32,200 portions but they had to divide it among all 52,700 members of their tribe, and this was the basis of their complaint.


###### Rashi on Joshua 17:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:14:2)


**עַד אֲשֶׁר־עַד־כֹּה בֵּרְכַנִי ה'** עַד אֲשֶׁר רָאִיתָ שֶׁנִּתְרַבָּה מִנְיָנוֹ מִמִּנְיָן רִאשׁוֹן לְמִנְיָן שֵׁנִי עֶשְׂרִים אֶלֶף וַחֲמֵשׁ מֵאוֹת כְּמִנְיַן 'כֹּה' בְּגִימְטַרְיָה, בְּמִנְיָן רִאשׁוֹן אַתָּה מוֹצֵא בִּמְנַשֶּׁה בְּסֵפֶר בְּמִדְבָּר (א לה): שְׁנַיִם וּשְׁלשִׁים אֶלֶף וּמָאתַיִם, וּבְפִינְחָס (שם כו לד): שְׁנַיִם וַחֲמִשִּׁים אֶלֶף וּשְׂבַע מֵאוֹת. דָּבָר אַחֵר: **עַד אֲשֶׁר עַד כֹּה** בְּרָכָה נֶאֶמְרָה לְאַבְרָהָם (בראשית טו ה): כֹּה יִהְיֶה זַרְעֲךָ, נִתְקַיְּימָה בִּי, וּלְפִי פְּשׁוּטוֹ: **עַד אֲשֶׁר עַד כֹּה** עַד אֲשֶׁר כָּךְ, כַּאֲשֶׁר אַתָּה רוֹאֶה.


**For until now so much has Adonoy blessed me.** For you have seen that my population has increased from the first counting to the second counting by twenty thousand, five hundred, which is the numerical equivilent of כּה (25). In the first counting you found the number of Menashe, stated in Seifer Bamidbar,⁷ to be thirty two thousand, two hundred, and in Parshes Pinchos⁸ their number was fifty two thousand, seven hundred. Another explanation is: until now, until כּה referring to the blessing that was said to Avrohom, “So (כּה) shall be your seed.”⁹ This blessing has been fulfilled in my tribe. And according to its plain meaning, until now-as I am now— until I am the multitude you see now.

footnotes:
⁷ Parshas Bamidbar 1:35.
⁸ Bamidbar 26:34.
⁹ Bereishis 15:5.

**לֹא יִמָּצֵא לָנוּ הָהָר** כִּי אָץ הוּא לָנוּ כַּאֲשֶׁר אָמַרְתָּ.

**וְרֶכֶב בַּרְזֶל** וּמַה שֶּׁאַתָּה אוֹמֵר לַעֲלוֹת הַיַּעְרָה וְלִכְבּוֹשׁ בְּאֶרֶץ הַפְּרִיזִּי וְהָרְפָאִים, עַם חָזָק הוּא אוֹתוֹ כְּנַעֲנִי וְרֶכֶב בַּרְזֶל לוֹ, וְאַל תִּתְמַהּ אִם קְרָאוֹ לַפְּרִיזִי וּרְפָאִים כְּנַעֲנִי, כִּי כֻּלַּם בְּנֵי כְּנַעַן הָיוּ.


**Iron chariots.** ‘And that what you say, go up to the forest and conquer the land of the Perezzites and the Rephaim [is not so simple] because the Canaanites are a mighty people, and they possess iron chariots.’ Do not wonder if Scripture refers to the Perezzites and Rephaim as Canaanites for they were all descendants of the original Canaan.¹²

### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Joshua 17:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:1)


**כִּי הַר יִהְיֶה־לָּךְ** אוֹתוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְתִּי לְךָ עֲלֵה לְךָ הַיַּעֲרָה.


**For the mountain will be yours.** The mountain of which I said to you “Go up to the forest.”


###### Rashi on Joshua 17:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:2)


**כִּי יַעַר הוּא וּבֵרֵאתוֹ** כִּי יַעַר הוּא וְאֵינוֹ רָאוּי אֶלָּא לְעַם רַב וִיבָרְאוּהוּ וִיפַנּוּהוּ.


**For it is a forest and you will cut it down.** For it is a forest and it is only fitting for a multitude of people, that will cut it down and clear it.


###### Rashi on Joshua 17:18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:3)


**וּבֵרֵאתוֹ** אַתָּה שֶׁאַתָּה עַם רַב.


**And you will cut it down.** You who are a multitude of people.


###### Rashi on Joshua 17:18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:4)


**וְהָיָה לְךָ תֹּצְאֹתָיו כִּי תוֹרִיש אֶת הַכְּנַעֲנִי** עַל יְדֵי שֶׁאַתָּה עַם רַב.


**And as far as it extends will be yours.** For you will drive out the Canaanites. Because you are a multitude of people.


###### Rashi on Joshua 17:18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:5)


**כִּי רֶכֶב בַּרְזֶל לוֹ** וְאֵין אֶחָד מִשְּׁאָר הַשְּׁבָטִים כְּדַאי לְהִלָּחֵם בּוֹ.


**Even though they possess iron chariots.** None of the other tribes have sufficient man power to fight them.


###### Rashi on Joshua 17:18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/17:18:6)


**כִּי חָזָק הוּא** וְאַתָּה יֵשׁ בְּךָ הַיְכוֹלֶת וְתּוֹרִישֶׁנּוּ, וְרַבּוֹתֵינוּ פֵּירְשׁוּ (בבא בתרא קיח א): עֲלֵה לְךָ הַיַּעְרָה, הַחְבִּיאוּ עַצְמְכֶם בַּיְעָרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא תִּשְׁלוֹט בָּכֶם עַיִן הָרָע.


**Even though they are stong.** You have the ability and will drive them out. Our Rabbis explained [the meaning of] ‘Go up to the forest— Hide yourselves in the forest and the “evil eye” will have no power over you.’¹³

footnotes:
¹³ Yehoshua implied that the forest would protect them against the “evil eye.” Because they were so numerous and multiplied so rapidly, they would invoke jealousy from their neighbors. It was therefore to their advantage to live in a forest area where they would not be so visible. If they would live in the cities of the plains, everyone would see how numerous they were, but in the forest they would not be as visible.



## Chapter 18


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:1:1)


**וַיַשְׁכִּינוּ שָׁם אֶת־אֹהֶל מוֹעֵד** שֶׁעָשׂוּ בַּמִּדְבָּר, וְלֹא הָיָה שָׁם תִּקְרָה, אֶלָּא בַּיִת שֶׁל אֲבָנִים מִלְּמַטָּן וִירִיעוֹת מִלְמַעְלָן, כָּךְ שָׁנִינוּ בִּשְׁחִיטַת קָדָשִׁים (זבחים כד ב).


**And there they made a dwelling place for the Tent of Meeting.** [This refers to the Tent of Meeting] that they had constructed in the wilderness.¹ It did not have a roof, but it was a stone structure on the bottom and was covered with curtains above.² remained at Gilgal. They had been too occupied with conquering and dividing the land to establish a permanent dwelling place for the Tent of Meeting. Once they began settling in the land, they established a House of God. The walls of the House were made of stone, and instead of a roof it was covered with curtains of cloth and skins, as it had been when it was constructed in the wilderness. This we learned in Maseches Zevachim.

footnotes:
¹ Since Scripture does not identify the Tent of Meeting, Rashi explains that it is the same one they had constructed in the wilderness.
² Zevachim 24b. During the fourteen years since they entered Eretz Yisroel, the Mishkan


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:1:2)


**וְהָאָרֶץ נִכְבְּשָׁה לִפְנֵיהֶם** מִשֶּׁנִּקְבַּע הַמִּשְׁכָּן, הָיְתָה הָאָרֶץ נוֹחָה לִיכָּבֵשׁ לִפְנֵיהֶם.


**And the land had been conquered before them.** Once the *Mishkon* was established, it became easier for them to conquer the land.³ they would be blessed with continued victory in the remaining battles of conquest.

footnotes:
³ The land had actually not been conquered at that point in time, but even though the conquest had not been completed, they had faith that in the merit of the Mishkan,



### Verse 2

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:2:1)


**שִׁבְעָה שְׁבָטִים** שֶׁכְּבָר קִבְּלוּ נַחֲלָתָן רְאוּבֵן וְגָד וַחֲצִי שֵׁבֶט מְנַשֶּׁה בִּימֵי משֶׁה בְּעֵבֶר הַיַּרְדֵּן, וּבְאֶרֶץ כְּנַעַן כְּבָר נָפַל גּוֹרָל לִיהוּדָה וּלְאֶפְרַיִם וְלַחֲצִי שֵׁבֶט מְנַשֶּׁה, הֲרֵי חֲמִשָּׁה שְׁבָטִים.


**Seven tribes.** [For the following tribes] had already received their portions: Reuvein, Gad, and the half tribe of Menashe had received their inheritance in the days of Moshe on the other side of the Jordan. In the land of Canaan itself, the lot had already fallen for Yehudah, Ephraim and the half tribe of Menashe. These five tribes were provided for.



### Verse 3

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:3:1)


**מִתְרַפִּים** מִתְרַשְּׁלִין.


**Timid**—weak.



### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:4:1)


**לַשָּׁבֶט** לְכָל שֵׁבֶט מִן הַשִּׁבְעָה.


**For each tribe.** For each of the remaining seven tribes.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:4:2)


**לְפִי נַחֲלָתָם** לְשִׁבְעָה חֲלָקִים וְלֹא בְּשָׁוֶה, אֶלָּא לְפִי הַשְּׁבָטִים הָרְאוּיִין לְחֶלְקָם, לָרַב לְפִי רֻבּוֹ וְלַמְּעַט לְפִי מִיעוּטוֹ, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר כו נד): לָרַב תַּרְבֶּה נַחֲלָתוֹ וְלַמְּעַט תַּמְעִיט נַחֲלָתוֹ וְגוֹמֵר.


**According to their inheritance.** Into seven parts but not equal ones, but according to the tribes appropriate portions— to the large tribe according to its largeness, and to the small tribe according to its smallness, as it is said, ‘To the large [tribe], increase their inheritance.’⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Bamidbar 26:54.



### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:5:1)


**וְהִתְחַלְּקוּ אֹתָהּ לְשִׁבְעָה חֲלָקִים** אוֹתָהּ שֶׁנִכְבְּשָׁה כְּבָר וְהָעֲתִידָה לִיכָּבֵשׁ.


**And they will divide it into seven parts.** Including that which was already conquered and that which was destined to be conquered.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:5:2)


**יְהוּדָה יַעֲמֹד עַל גְּבוּלוֹ מִנֶּגֶב** אֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁשָּׁם נָפַל לוֹ גּוֹרָל כָּל הַמֶּצֶר הַדְּרוֹמִי.


**Yehudah will remain at his boundary in the south**— of Eretz Yisroel, for there fell to his lot the entire southern boundary.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:5:3)


**וּבֵית יוֹסֵף וְגוֹ' מִצָּפוֹן** צְפוֹנִי לְמַה שֶּׁנִּכְבַּשׁ כְּבָר, וְעוֹד יֵשׁ הַרְבֵּה לִכְבּוֹשׁ צַד הַמֶּצֶר מִבַּעַל גַּד עַד לְבוֹא חֲמָת, שֶׁהוּא אֵצֶל הֹר הָהָר בְּמֶצֶר צְפוֹנִי, וְהַשִּׁבְעָה שְׁבָטִים יְחַלְּקוּ אוֹתָהּ שֶׁבֵּין יְהוּדָה לְיוֹסֵף, וְאוֹתָהּ הָעוֹמֶדֶת לִיכָּבֵשׁ.


**And the house of Yoseif—in the north.** The north of the land already conquered. There is however much more to be conquered: the side of the boundary from Baal-gad to Levo-chamos which is near Hor hoHor in the northern boundary. The seven tribes will divide [the land] between Yehudah and Yoseif, and [the land] which remains to be conquered.



### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:6:1)


**וְיָרִיתִי** וְהִשְׁלַכְתִּי, כְּמוֹ (שמות טו ד): יָרָה בַיָּם.


**I will cast.**—I will throw, similar to, ‘He threw into the sea.’⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Shemos 15:4.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:12:1)


**וַיְהִי לָהֶם הַגְּבוּל לִפְאַת צָפוֹנָה** וַיְהִי מֶצֶר גְּבוּל הַצְּפוֹנִי שֶׁלָּהֶם מִן הַיַּרְדֵּן, שֶׁבַּמִּזְרָח מַתְחִיל הַמֶּצֶר, וְעָלָה אֶל כֶּתֶף יְרִיחוֹ מִצָּפוֹן, וְעָלָה מִשָּׁם אֶל הַמַּעֲרָב אֶל עֵבֶר יְרִיחוֹ, וּמוֹשֵׁךְ הַחוּט בִּצְפוֹנָהּ שֶׁל יְרִיחוֹ, נִמְצֵאת יְרִיחוֹ לִפְנִים מִן הַחוּט בְּחֵלֶק בִּנְיָמִין.


**Their northern boundary.** Their northern boundary was the Jordan, since the border started from the east and went up alongside Yericho from the north. It went from there westward alongside Yericho and the border line is drawn on the northside of Yericho. Thus Yericho is within the border line in the portion of Binyomin.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:12:2)


**מִדְבָּרָהּ בֵּית אָוֶן** לַמִּדְבַּר שֶׁל בֵּית אָוֶן.


**To the wilderness of Beis-Oven.** To the wilderness of Beis-Oven.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ Rashi explains that the text מִדְבְּרָה בֵּית אָוֶן is the same as לַמִדְבַּר שֶׁל בֵּית אָוֶן.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:13:1)


**וְעָבַר מִשָּׁם** לְצַד הַמַּעֲרָב.


**From there the boundary passed**—westward.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:13:2)


**לוּזָה אֶל כֶּתֶף לוּזָה נֶגְבָּה הִיא בֵּית אֵל** שֶׁקְּרָאָהּ יַעֲקֹב (בראשית כח יט), וְלֹא זוֹ הִיא בֵּית אֵל שֶׁאֵצֶל הָעַי, שֶׁהֲרֵי אוֹמֵר בִּגְבוּל יוֹסֵף (לעיל טז ב): וְיָצָא מִבֵּית אֵל לוּזָה, לָמַדְנוּ שֶׁשְּׁנַיִם הָיוּ, וְלוּז לֹא בְּחֵלֶק בִּנְיָמִין הָיְתָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי חוּט הַמֶּצֶר מִלּוּז וְלִפְנִים הוּא, כְּמוֹ שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר (פסוק יג): אֶל כֶּתֶף לוּזָה נֶגְבָּה, הַחוּט הוֹלֵךְ בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל לוּז, נִמְצֵאת לוּז מִן הַחוּט וְלַחוּץ, בְּנַחֲלַת בְּנֵי יוֹסֵף, וּבֵית אֵל הָיְתָה בְּחֵלֶק בִּנְיָמִין, כְּמוֹ שֶׁכָּתוּב לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן (להלן פסוק כב).


**To Luz, the southside of Luz which is Beis El.** [This is the city] that Yaakov called [Beis-el.] It is not the Beis-el which is near Ai, for it is said about the boundary of Yoseif: ‘And it extended from Beis-el to Luz.’⁷ Thus we learn that there were two [Beis-els]. Luz was not in the portion of Binyomin; for the boundary line was from Luz and within; as it is said: ‘To the southside of Luz.’ the boundary line goes south of Luz, thus Luz is outside the boundary line, and is within the inheritance of the Bnei Yoseif. Beis-el is in the portion of Binyomin; as it said later in this section.⁸

footnotes:
⁷ Chapter 16:2.
⁸ See Verse 22.



### Verse 14

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:14:1)


**וְתָאַר הַגְּבוּל וְנָסַב לִפְאַת יָם נֶגְבָּה** בְּעַטְרוֹת אַדָּר כָּלָה אוֹרֶךְ הַצְּפוֹנִי מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב, וּמִשָּׁם נָסַב הַחוּט לִגְבוּל הַמַּעֲרָב שֶׁל בִּנְיָמִין לֵילֵךְ נֶגְבָּה מִן הַצָּפוֹן לַדָּרוֹם, חוּט הַמַּעֲרָבִית מִן הָהָר אֲשֶׁר עַל פְּנֵי בֵּית חוֹרוֹן נֶגְבָּה מִן הָהָר אֲשֶׁר בִּדְרוֹמָהּ שֶׁל בֵּית חוֹרוֹן, מִשָּׁם הָיָה מַתְחִיל מִקְצוֹעַ מַעֲרָבִית צְפוֹנִית שֶׁל בִּנְיָמִין וּבָא לוֹ הַחוּט מִצָּפוֹן לְדָרוֹם.


**And the boundary circled and went around the western side southward.** At Atros-ador, the length of the northern boundary ended; it went from east to west. From there the boundary line encircled the western boundary of Binyomin to go southward from north to south. The western boundary line, extended from the mountain which was opposite Beis-choron— south of the mountain— which was south of Beis-choron; that was the beginning of the northwest corner of Binyomen’s [portion,] and the boundary line went from north to south.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:14:2)


**וְהָיוּ תֹצְאֹתָיו** מִקְצוֹעַ מַעֲרָבִית דְּרוֹמִית שֶׁלּוֹ אֶל קִרְיַת יְעָרִים שֶׁל יְהוּדָה, נִמְצָא רוֹחַב גְּבוּל בִּנְיָמִין נוֹטֵל כָּל רוֹחַב הָאָרֶץ שֶׁבֵּין יְהוּדָה לְאֶפְרַיִם, גְּבוּל דְּרוֹמִי שֶׁלּוֹ עִם גְּבוּל צְפוֹנִי שֶׁל יְהוּדָה, וְשָׁם הָיְתָה יְרוּשָׁלַיִם כְּמוֹ שֶׁאָמוּר לְמַטָּה בָּעִנְיָן, לְפִיכָךְ הָיָה לִשְׁנֵי הַשְּׁבָטִים חֵלֶק בָּהּ, וּגְבוּל צְפוֹנִי שֶׁל בִּנְיָמִין וּדְרוֹמִי שֶׁל אֶפְרַיִם נוֹגְעִין זֶה בָּזֶה, וְשָׁם הָיְתָה שִׁילֹה בְּחֵלֶק אֶפְרַיִם, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (תהלים עח ס סז): וַיִּטֹּשׁ מִשְׁכַּן שִׁילֹה וְגוֹמֵר וַיִּמְאַס בְּאֹהֶל יוֹסֵף וּבְשֵׁבֶט אֶפְרַיִם לֹא בָחָר, וְאַף לְבִנְיָמִין הָיָה בּוֹ חֵלֶק, וְכֵן שָׁנִינוּ בִּשְׁחִיטַת קָדָשִׁים (זבחים כד ב): בְּג' מְקוֹמוֹת שָׁרְתָה לָהֶם שְׁכִינָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל, בְּשִׁילֹה וְנוֹב וּבֵית הָעוֹלָמִים, וּבְכֻלָּן לֹא שָׁרְתָה אֶלָּא בְּחֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל בִּנְיָמִין.


**It extended—** From its south-western corner to Kiryas-yearim which belonged to Yehudah. Thus the width of Binyomin’s boundary occupies the entire width of the land between Yehudah and Ephraim. His [Binyomin’s] southern boundary meets with the northern boundary of Yehudah, which was the location of *Yerushalayim*, as is said later in this chapter.⁹ Thus both tribes had a share in it.¹⁰. (See v. 28) The city and the *Beis ha Mikdosh* were situated right on the border of the two tribes. There are various opinions regarding the exact location of the Altar itself. Some say that it was in Binyomin’s portion, while others maintain that it was in the portion of Yehudah. The Northern boundary of Binyomin and the sourthern boundary of Ephraim meet, and Shiloh was located there in the portion of Ephraim, as it is said, ‘And He abandoned the Mishkan of Shiloh, etc., and He abhorred the tent of Yoseif, and the tribe of Ephraim He did not choose.’¹¹ Binyomin too had a share in it [Shiloh], and so we learned in Maseches Zevachim, ‘In three places the Divine Presence rested in Eretz Yisroel: in Shiloh, in Nov and in the Beis ha Mikdosh; and of all of them, it rested only in the portion of Binyomin:¹² where the Beis ha Mikdosh was built. The reason for the pre-eminence of Binyomin in this respect, was the blessing Moshe gave to him, as it is said: “Adonoy’s friend, let him live secure with Him. He hovers protectively over him throughout the day, and He resides between his shoulders.” (Devorim 33:12) The *Shechina,* Divine Presence, resided in Shiloh for many years, and for a short time it *hovered* over Nov and Givon, and finally a permanent, eternal dwelling place for the *Shechina* was established in *Yerushalayim*. Thus in his blessing, Moshe alluded to the places within the territory of Binyomin in which the Divine Presence would reside. (See Rashi there).

footnotes:
⁹ V. 28.
¹⁰ The major portion of Yehudah’s territory was to the south, but there was one strip of land that extended north and bordered the portion of Binyomin. It extended to the city of the Yevusim which later became Yerushalayim
¹¹ Tehillim 78:60,67.
¹² From the time the land was divided, the Divine Presence (שְׁכִינָה) rested within the portion of Binyomin. Initially the Divine Presence rested in the Mishkon at Shiloh. After its destruction, the Mishkon was established at Nov, which was also within Binyomin’s portion. It was moved from there to Givon and afterwards to Yerushalayim


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:14:3)


**זֹאת פְּאַת יָם** רוּחַ מַעֲרָבִי מֵעַטְרוֹת אַדָּר עַד קִרְיַת יְעָרִים.


**This was the western side—** the west side extending from Atros-ador to Kiryas-yearim.



### Verse 15

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:15:1)


**וּפְאַת נֶגְבָּה** מִצַּד דְּרוֹמִי לְבִנְיָמִין וְהוּא צְפוֹנִי שֶׁל יְהוּדָה, וְכָל הַתְּחוּמִין הַמְּנוּיִין כָּאן נִמְנוּ בִּצְפוֹנָהּ שֶׁל יְהוּדָה, וְכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁכָּתוּב כָּאן 'וְיָרַד', כָּתוּב בִּיהוּדָה 'וְעָלָה', לְפִי שֶׁכָּאן הוּא מוֹנֶה מִן הַמַּעֲרָב לַמִּזְרָח, וְשָׁם מוֹנֶה מִן הַמִּזְרָח לַמַּעֲרָב.


**And the southern side—** Referring to the southern border of Binyomin which is identical with the northern border of Yehudah. All the borders that are listed here are listed relative to the north of Yehudah, and every place described here as descending, is described in reference to Yehudah as going up because here he is listing [boundaries] from west to east and there he lists them from east to west.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:15:2)


**וְיָצָא הַגְּבוּל יָמָּה** אֶל יָם אֶחָד וְלֹא יָדַעְתִּי אֵיזֶה יָם הוּא.


**And the boundary extended to the sea—** to a sea, but I do not know which sea it is.



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:19:1)


**אֶל לְשׁוֹן יַם הַמֶּלַח צָפוֹנָה** בִּצְפוֹנוֹ שֶׁל לָשׁוֹן, נִמְצָא כָּל הַלָּשׁוֹן בְּחֶלְקוֹ שֶׁל יְהוּדָה.


**To the bay of the Salt Sea to the north.** To the north of the bay. Thus the entire bay was in the portion of Yehudah.


###### Rashi on Joshua 18:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:19:2)


**אֶל קְצֵה הַיַּרְדֵּן** מָקוֹם שֶׁהַיַּרְדֵּן נוֹפֵל בְּיַם הַמֶּלַח, וְהוּא נִמְנֶה מִקְצוֹעַ מִזְרָחִית צְפוֹנִית לִיהוּדָה, וְכָאן הוּא מוֹנֶה מִקְצוֹעַ דְּרוֹמִית מִזְרָחִית לְבִנְיָמִין.


**[To the southern] end of the Jordan.** Referring to the place where the Jordan falls [empties into] the Dead Sea. This was listed as the north-eastern edge [of the territory] of Yehudah. And here it is listed as the south-eastern edge [of the territory] of Binyomin.



### Verse 20

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:20:1)


**וְהַיַּרְדֵּן יִגְבֹּל־אֹתוֹ לִפְאַת קֵדְמָה** הַיַּרְדֵּן הָיָה לוֹ לְבִנְיָמִין מֶצֶר מִזְרָחִי, שֶׁהַיַּרְדֵּן הוֹלֵךְ עַל פְּנֵי רוֹחַב כָּל גְּבוּלוֹ לְמִזְרָח.


**The Jordan borders it on the east.** The Jordan was the eastern boundary of Binyomin; as the Jordan goes along the width of his entire boundary to the east.



### Verse 28

###### Rashi on Joshua 18:28:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:28:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/18:28:1)


**וְצֵלַע הָאֶלֶף וְהַיְבוּסִי הִיא יְרוּשָׁלַיִם** כָּל אַחַת עִיר לְעַצְמָהּ, וְכֵן גִּבְעַת, קִרְיַת, חָמֵשׁ עָרִים בְּפָסוּק זֶה.


**Tzeilah, Eleph and Yevusi which is *Yerushalayim*.** Each of these are individual cities. Similarly, Givas and Kiryas [are individual cities]. Thus five cities are mentioned in this verse.



## Chapter 19


### Verse 1

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:1:1)


**וַיֵּצֵא הַגּוֹרָל הַשֵּׁנִי** שֵׁנִי לְבִנְיָמִין, שֶׁגּוֹרָל בִּנְיָמִין הָיָה רִאשׁוֹן לְשִׁבְעָה הַשְּׁבָטִים שֶׁאָמַר יְהוֹשֻׁעַ לִשְׁלוּחָיו וְהִתְחַלְּקוּ אוֹתָהּ לְשִׁבְעָה חֲלָקִים, שֶׁכְּבָר נָטְלוּ יְהוּדָה וְיוֹסֵף, כְּמוֹ שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר שָׁם (לעיל יח ה): יְהוּדָה יַעֲמֹד עַל גְּבוּלוֹ מִנֶּגֶב וְגוֹ', מִכָּאן וְאֵילָךְ מוֹנֶה וְהוֹלֵךְ ז' גּוֹרָלוֹת.


**The second lot came out.** It was second to that of Binyomin, for Binyomin’s lot was the first of the seven tribes whose representatives Yehoshua spoke to [saying]: “And they will divide it into seven parts,” for Yehudah and Yoseif had previously received [their inheritance] as it is said there: “Yehudah will remain at his boundary in the south,” [and the house of Yoseif will remain at their boundary in the north].”¹ From here he goes on to list the seven lots.

footnotes:
¹ See above 18:5.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:9:1)


**רַב מֵהֶם** מִן הָרָאוּי לָהֶם.


**Was too large for them.** It was more than was their due.²

footnotes:
² Various reasons are presented why Shimon was given part of their inheritance in the midst of Yehudah’s territory. Some say that Shimon helped Yehudah conquer their land and therefore merited to receive some of it as their own. Others maintain that this was because Yaakov had said: “Shimon and Levi are brothers, instruments of violence are their wares. I will disperse them throughout Yaakov and scatter them throughout Israel.” (Bereishis 49:5,7) Shimon was given a portion within Yehudah and Levi was scattered throughout many cities in the land.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:12:1)


**כִּסְלוֹת תָּבוֹר** אוֹמֵר אֲנִי שֶׁהוּא לְשׁוֹן כְּסָלִים, פלנק"ש בְּלַעַז (צַלְעוֹת הַבְּהֵמָה), [כּמוֹ וַיִקְרָא ג'ד'] לֹא בְּגוֹבְהוֹ וְלֹא בְּשִׁפּוּלוֹ אֶלָּא בְּשִׁפּוּעוֹ וְסָמוּךְ לְאֶמְצָעוֹ קָרוֹב לְצַד אֲחוֹרַיִם, וּמִלְּצַד פָּנָיו, כְּדֶרֶךְ שֶׁהַכְּסָלִים עוֹמְדוֹת בַּבְּהֵמָה, וּבְמָקוֹם שֶׁהוּא אוֹמֵר (להלן פסוק לד) אָזְנוֹת תָּבוֹר, סָמוּךְ לְרֹאשׁוֹ הוּא, בִּמְקוֹם הָאָזְנַיִם.


**Kislos-tovor.** I say that [כִּסְלוֹת] is an expression of flanks, כְּסָלִים, flancs in old French. This is similar to what is stated in Vayikro 3:4. It is not at its highest point and not at its bottom, but on its slope, near the middle, closer to the rear, than the front, like the flanks of an animal. [Thus in this verse it reads the flanks of Mt. Tabor]. However when Scripture states, Oznos-Tavor, it refers to a place near its top, the place where the ears are situated.



### Verse 13

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:13:1)


**קֵדְמָה מִזְרָחָה גִּתָּה חֵפֶר** מִמִּזְרָח לְגַת הַחֵפֶר, שֵׁם עִירוֹ שֶׁל יוֹנָה בֶּן אֲמִתַּי (מלכים ב יד כה).


**East of Gitoh-cheifer.** To the east of Gat-hacheifer which is the name of the city of [the prophet] Yonah ben Amtai.


###### Rashi on Joshua 19:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:13:2)


**עִתָּה קָצִין** שֵׁם הָעִיר עִתָּה קָצִין.


**Ittoh-kotzin.** This was the name of the city.


###### Rashi on Joshua 19:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:13:3)


**הַמְּתֹאָר הַנֵּעָה** הַמּוּסָב עַל נֵעָה, כְּלוֹמַר מִשָּׁם תָּאַר הַגְּבוּל לְנֵעָה, וְכֵן תִּרְגֵּם יוֹנָתָן: וְנָפֵיק לְרִמּוֹן וּמִתַּמָּן מִסְתְּחָר לְנֵעָה.


**It circled around to Neioh.** [From Rimmon] it circled to Neioh. In other words, from there, the boundary circled around to Neioh. Targum Yonason also explains it this way: “It extended to Rimmon, and from there it circled to Neioh.”³

footnotes:
³ Rashi goes to great pain to refute those who say that the word הַמְתאָר is connected to רִמוֹן and thus translate, Rimon-misoar as though this was the name of the city.



### Verse 29

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:29:1)


**מִבְצַר צֹר** (תַּרְגּוּם:) קִרְוִין כְּרִיכָן קִרְוֵי תּוּקְפָא.


**Fortified city of the Rock.** Cities of fortress, cities of might.


###### Rashi on Joshua 19:29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:29:2)


**צֹר** לְשׁוֹן צוּר.


**Rock**—צוּר means a rock.


###### Rashi on Joshua 19:29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:29:3)


**מֵחֶבֶל אַכְזִיבָה** (תַּרְגּוּם:) מֵעֲדַב אַכְזִיבָה, מִגּוֹרָל כְּזִיב.


**From the lot of Achziv.** [חֶבֶל means a rope of Achziv] but here it refers to the lot of Achziv.



### Verse 35

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:35:1)


**וְעָרֵי מִבְצָר** הָיוּ לְנַפְתָּלִי הַצִּדִּים צֵר.


**Fortified cities—** which belonged to Naftoli were Tzidim, Tzeir, etc.



### Verse 41

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:41:1)


**צָרְעָה וְאֶשְׁתָּאוֹל** מִשֶּׁל יְהוּדָה הָיָה (לעיל טו לג), וְנָפַל גּוֹרָל בְּנֵי דָּן סָמוּךְ לָהֶם.


**Tzoroh, Eshtaeil.** These were cities in the portion of Yehudah, and the lot of Bnei Don fell near them.



### Verse 47

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:47:1)


**וַיֵּצֵא גְּבוּל בְּנֵי־דָן־מֵהֶם** כָּאן נָטְלוּ קְצָת וְעוֹד נָפַל לָהֶם גּוֹרָל בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר רָחוֹק מִגְּבוּלָם, וּשְׁאָר שְׁבָטִים מַפְסִיקִין בֵּינְתַיִם.


**The boundary of Bnei Don extended from them.** At this point they took only a small portion, but the lot also fell for them in another place which was a distance from their boundary, and thus the other tribes intervened between them.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Others explain the words, וַיֵצֵא גְבוּל בְנֵי־דָן מֵהֶם to mean, “the boundary of the Bnei Don was too small for them,” and they went out to capture the city of Leshem to make room for their population.


###### Rashi on Joshua 19:47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:47:2)


**וַיִּלָּחֲמוּ עִם לֶשֶׁם** לְאַחַר זְמַן בִּימֵי עָתְנִיאֵל בֶּן קְנַז, וּבִימֵי פֶּסֶל מִיכָה, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר בְּסֵפֶר שׁוֹפְטִים (יח ב).


**They fought against Leshem.** This took place later in the time of Osniel, the son of Kenaz, in the days of the graven image of Michoh, as it is said in the Book of Shoftim.⁵

footnotes:
⁵ Shoftim 17:3,4. 18:2. (See Rashi to 17:1.)


###### Rashi on Joshua 19:47:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:47:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:47:3)


**לֶשֶׁם** הִיא לַיִשׁ הָאֲמוּרָה בְּסֵפֶר שׁוֹפְטִים (יח יד).


**Leshem** This is the city of Layish, that is mentioned in the Book of Shoftim.⁶

footnotes:
⁶ Shoftim 18:7,14.



### Verse 51

###### Rashi on Joshua 19:51:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:51:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/19:51:1)


**אֲשֶׁר נָחֲלוּ** הִנְחִילוּ.


**That were divided for inheritance.** They caused them to inherit.



## Chapter 20


### Verse 6

###### Rashi on Joshua 20:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/20:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/20:6:1)


**עַד עָמְדוֹ וְגוֹ' לַמִּשְׁפָּט** אִם יִפָּטֵר מִגָּלוּת יִפָּטֵר, וְאִם יִתְחַיֵּיב גָּלוּת יַחֲזוֹר לְעִיר מִקְלָטוֹ (במדבר לה נה): וְיֵשֵׁב שָׁם עַד מוֹת הַכֹּהֵן הַגָּדוֹל.


**Until he stands, etc. for judgement.** If he is acquited from exile, he is discharged. If he is sentanced to exile, he is returned to the city of his refuge, and he must reside there until the death of the *Kohein-godol.*



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Joshua 20:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/20:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/20:8:1)


**וּמֵעֵבֶר לְיַרְדֵּן יְרִיחוֹ מִזְרָחָה נָתְנוּ** בִּימֵי משֶׁה, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (דברים ד מג): אֶת בֶּצֶר בַּמִּדְבָּר וְגוֹ'.


**And on the other side of the Jordan, from Yericho eastward.** [These cities were set aside] during the days of Moshe, as it is said, “Betzer in the wilderness.”¹

footnotes:
¹ Devorim 4:43.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 20:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/20:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/20:9:1)


**הַמּוּעָדָה** הַהַזְמָנָה הַיְעוּדוֹת לְכָךְ.


**That were set aside.** They were prepared and set aside for this.



## Chapter 21


### Verse 5

###### Rashi on Joshua 21:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:5:1)


**וְלִבְנֵי קְהָת הַנּוֹתָרִים** הֵם בְּנֵי משֶׁה וּבְנֵי יִצְהָר וְחֶבְרוֹן וְעֻזִּיאֵל.


**And the remaining descendants of Kehos.** They were the descendants of Moshe, the descendants of Yitzhar, Chevron and Uzziel.¹

footnotes:
¹ Shemos 6:18.


###### Rashi on Joshua 21:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:5:2)


**מִמִּשְׁפַּחַת מַטֵּה אֶפְרַיִם** מִנַּחֲלַת שֵׁבֶט בְּנֵי אֶפְרַיִם נָפְלוּ לָהֶם אֵלֶּה הֶעָרִים, שֶׁבְּכָל שֵׁבֶט וְשֵׁבֶט נָטְלוּ, כְּמָה שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (במדבר לה ח): מֵאֵת הָרַב תַּרְבּוּ וּמֵאֵת הַמְּעַט תַּמְעִיטוּ אִישׁ כְּפִי נַחֲלָתוֹ אֲשֶׁר יִנְחָלוּ יִתֵּן מֵעָרָיו לַלְוִיִּם.


**From the families of the tribe of Ephraim.** From the inheritance of the tribe of the descendants of Ephraim, these cities were alloted to them. They received cities from the portion of each tribe, as it is said: “Take more from a larger [portion], and less from a smaller [portion]; each person according to his inheritance, shall give of his cities to the *Levi’im.*²

footnotes:
² Bamidbar 35:8.



### Verse 9

###### Rashi on Joshua 21:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:9:1)


**אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא אֶתְהֶן בְּשֵׁם** לְפִי שֶׁלֹּא הִזְכִּיר לְמַעְלָה אֶת שְׁמוֹת הֶעָרִים שֶׁנִּתְּנוּ לָהֶם, וּלְמַטָּה הוּא מְפָרֵשׁ אֶת שְׁמוֹתָם, לְכָךְ אָמַר: אֲשֶׁר יִקְרָא אֶתְהֶן בְּשֵׁם.


**That will be mentioned by name.** Since he had not mentioned earlier the names of the cities that were given to them, and he enumerates their names later, he therefore states [now], “these cities that *will* be mentioned by name.”



### Verse 18

###### Rashi on Joshua 21:18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:18:1)


**וְאֶת עַלְמוֹן** וּבְדִבְרֵי הַיָּמִים (דברי הימים א ו מה): וְאֶת עָלֶמֶת, הִיא בַּחוּרִים, שֶׁיּוֹנָתָן (שמואל ב ג טז) מְתַרְגֵּם אוֹתָהּ: עֲלָמוֹת.


**And Almon.** In Sefer Divrei haYomim it is rendered: and Olemes this is similar to Bachurim which Yonason translates as Alomos.³

³ Both words בַחוּרִים and עַלָמוֹת refer to youth.


###### Rashi on Joshua 21:18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/21:18:2)


**אֶת מִגְרָשֶׁיהָ סְבִיבוֹתֶיהָ** אַלְפַּיִם אַמָּה לְכָל רוּחַ.


**and their open spaces around it.** Two thousand *amos* on each side.



## Chapter 22


### Verse 7

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:7:1)


**וְגַם כִּי שִׁלְּחָם יְהוֹשֻׁעַ** לַחֲצִי שֵׁבֶט הַמְנַשֶּׁה, כַּאֲשֶׁר שָׁלַח אֶת בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן וְגָד.


**Furthermore, when Yehoshua sent them off.** He sent off the half-tribe of Menashe in the same manner as he sent off the Bnei Reuvein and the Bnei Gad.



### Verse 8

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:8:1)


**עִם אֲחֵיכֶם** בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן וּבְנֵי גָּד, וְיֵשׁ פּוֹתְרִין: **עִם אֲחֵיכֶם** שֶׁנִּשְׁאֲרוּ לִשְׁמוֹר אֶת הֶעָרִים עִם הַנָּשִׁים וְהַטַּף, וְלֹא עָבְרוּ אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן עִם הַחֲלוּצִים, גַּם הֵם נָטְלוּ חֵלֶק בַּבִּזָּה.


**[Divide the spoils] with your brothers—** Referring to Bnei Gad and Bnei Reuvein. There are some who interpret “brothers” in referrence to those who remained behind to guard the cities and the women and children. [Even though] they did not cross the Jordan— with the armed division, nevertheless they received a share of the spoils.¹

footnotes:
¹ This practice was instituted by Avrohom. See Bereishis 14:24. Rashi comments there that Dovid learned from Avrohom’s action and established this as a tradition.



### Verse 12

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:12:1)


**לַעֲלוֹת עֲלֵיהֶם לַצָּבָא** לְפִי שֶׁנֶּאֶסְרוּ הַבָּמוֹת מִשֶּׁנִּקְבַּע הַמִּשְׁכָּן בְּשִׁילֹה.


**To go up against them to war.** [They were prepared to do this] because בָּמוֹת were prohibited² or the *Beis ha Mikdosh.* These בָּמוֹת were permitted during the period before the establishment of the Mishkan, but were prohibited so long as the Mishkan was intact. They were considered a threat to the unity of the Klal Yisroel and to the centrality of the Mishkan. once the *Mishkan* was established at Shiloh.

footnotes:
² The term בָּמוֹת is used to describe any type of altar used for sacrifices except the altar of the Mishkan



### Verse 19

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:19:1)


**אִם טְמֵאָה אֶרֶץ אֲחֻזַּתְכֶם** שֶׁלֹּא בָּחַר הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא לְהַשְׁרוֹת בָּהּ שְׁכִינָתוֹ.


**If the land of Your posession is impure.** Since the Holy blessed is He, did not choose to rest His Presence there.


###### Rashi on Joshua 22:19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:19:2)


**וְאֹתָנוּ אַל תִּמְרֹדוּ** כְּמוֹ: וּבָנוּ אַל תִּמְרוֹדוּ.


**Do not rebel against us.**—וְאוֹתָנוּ אַל תִּמְרדוּ This is the same as—וּבָּנוּ אַל תִּמְרדוּ



### Verse 22

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:22:1)


**אֵל אֱלֹהִים ה'** אֵל כָּל הָאֱלֹהִים הוּא ה' הַיּוֹדֵעַ כִּי לֹא בְּמֶרֶד וְגוֹמֵר, וּכְשֶׁכָּפַל לוֹמַר שְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים אֱלֹהִים, בָּעוֹלָם הַזֶּה וּבָעוֹלָם הַבָּא.


**Almighty God is Adonoy.** He is God over all divine beings. He is Adonoy, Who knows that our intent was not rebellion etc. By repeating this declaration twice, [they declared] that He is God in this world and in the World to Come.


###### Rashi on Joshua 22:22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:22:2)


**אַל תּוֹשִיעֵנוּ** כְּלַפֵּי שְׁכִינָה אָמְרוּ.


**Do not save us this day.** They said this to His Divine Presence.



### Verse 23

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:23:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:23:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:23:1)


**הוּא יְבַקֵּשׁ** יִפָּרַע מִמֶּנּוּ.


**Let Him prosecute**—Let him punish us.



### Verse 24

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:24:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:24:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:24:1)


**מִדְּאָגָה מִדָּבָר** מֵחֲמַת דַּאֲגַת יִרְאַת דְּבַר חֶרְפָּה עָשִׂינוּ, כְּמָה שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ פֶּן יֹאמְרוּ בְּנֵיכֶם מָחָר לְחָרֵף אֶת בָּנֵינוּ, כְּשֶׁיֵּלְכוּ לְהַקְרִיב בְּמִשְכַּן שִׁילֹה, שֶׁמָּא יֹאמְרוּ מַה לָּכֶם וְלַה', וַהֲלֹא גְּבוּל נָתַן בֵּינֵינוּ וּבֵינֵיכֶם אֶת הַיַּרְדֵּן, זוֹ דָּאַגְנוּ וְעָשִׂינוּ. כָּל 'דְּאָגָה' שֶׁבַּמִּקְרָא לְשׁוֹן יִרְאָה הוּא, כְּמוֹ (ירמיהו לח יט): אֲנִי דּוֹאֵג אֶת הַיְּהוּדִים, דְּצִדְקִיָּהוּ.


**Our anxiety of this.** Because of anxiety, and the fear of insulting words, have we done this, as is further explained in the Scripture: ‘Lest tomorrow your children will say,’ Lest they insult our children— when they go to bring sacrifices in the *Mishkan* at Shiloh— lest they say to them, ‘What have you to do with Adonoy?’ Did he not set a boundary between us and you, namely, the Jordan. This was our fear and thus we made it. Every word, דְּאָגָה, found in Scripture expresses fear, similar to, “I am concerned [fear] that the Jews etc.” [These were the words] of Tzidkiahu.³

footnotes:
³ See Yirmiahu 38:19. King Tzidkiahu was concerned that the Jews that had been captured by the Kasdim would betray him and he too would fall into their hands.



### Verse 27

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:27:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:27:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:27:1)


**כִּי עֵד הוּא** שֶׁלֹּא סִילַּקְנוּ עַצְמֵנוּ מִתּוֹרַת מִזְבֵּחַ.


**Rather it will bear witness** that we have not withdrawn ourselves from the law governing the altar.



### Verse 33

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:33:1)


**וַיְבָרְכוּ אֱלֹהִים** (תַּרְגּוּם:) וְאוֹדִיאוּ קֳדָם ה' בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל.


**And they the Bnei Yisroel blessed God.** The Bnei Yisroel thanked Adonoy.⁴

footnotes:
⁴ Rashi here quotes Targum Yonasan, who feels that it is more appropriate to say that they thanked God, rather than blessed Him.



### Verse 34

###### Rashi on Joshua 22:34:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:34:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/22:34:1)


**וַיִּקְרְאוּ בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן וּבְנֵי גָד לַמִּזְבֵּחַ כִּי עֵד הוּא** הֲרֵי זֶה מִן הַמִּקְרָאוֹת הַקְּצָרִים, וְצָרִיךְ לְהוֹסִיף בּוֹ תֵּיבָה אַחַת: וַיִּקְרְאוּ בְּנֵי רְאוּבֵן וּבְנֵי גָד לַמִּזְבֵּחַ עֵד.


**And the Bnei Reuvein and Bnei Gad called the altar, for it is a witness.** This is one of the shortened verses. One more word should have been added: And the Bnei Reuven and Bnei Gad called the altar ‘*witness,*’ [for it is etc.]



## Chapter 23


### Verse 4

###### Rashi on Joshua 23:4:1

**רְאוּ הִפַּלְתִּי לָכֶם** בְּגוֹרָל לְנַחֲלָה.


**You have seen how I have alloted to you.** By means of the lot for your inheritance.


###### Rashi on Joshua 23:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/23:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Rashi%20on%20Joshua/r/23:4:2)


**הַנִּשְׁאָרִים** לִיכָּבֵשׁ.
